 And when Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, accepted Adam's repentance, He did not lower him from the rank of vicegerency and Imamate that He had established for them.

 وَلَا أَخْلَاهُ مِنْ مَادَّتِهِ وَكَلِمَتِهِ وَحِكْمَتِهِ بِٱلْكُلِّيَّةِ،

 

 Nor did He empty him entirely of his substance and word and wisdom.

 وَلَٰكِنَّهُ أَهْبَطَهُ وَحَوَّاءَ مَعَهُ مِنْ حُدُودِ ٱلتَّأْوِيلِ بِدُونِ حِجَابٍ،

 

 But He sent him down, and Eve with him, from the boundaries of interpretation without a veil.

 فَقَالَ: ﴿اهْبِطُوا بَعْضُكُمْ لِبَعْضٍ عَدُوٌّ وَلَكُمْ فِي الْأَرْضِ مُسْتَقَرٌّ وَمَتَاعٌ إِلَى حِينٍ﴾

 

 And said: "Descend, [some of you] being enemies to one another. And for you on the earth is a place of settlement and enjoyment for a time."

 وَقَالَ: ﴿فِيهَا تَحْيَوْنَ وَفِيهَا تَمُوتُونَ وَمِنْهَا تُخْرَجُونَ﴾

 

 And He said: "Therein you will live, and therein you will die, and from it you will be brought forth."

 وَقَالَ: ﴿يَا بَنِي آدَمَ قَدْ أَنْزَلْنَا عَلَيْكُمْ لِبَاسًا يُوَارِي سَوْآتِكُمْ وَرِيشًا وَلِبَاسُ التَّقْوَىٰ ذَٰلِكَ خَيْرٌ ذَٰلِكَ مِنْ آيَاتِ اللَّهِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَذَّكَّرُونَ﴾

 

 And He said: "O children of Adam, We have bestowed upon you clothing to conceal your private parts and as adornment. But the clothing of righteousness - that is best. That is from the signs of Allah that perhaps they will remember."

 

 

 فَأَهْبَطَ ٱللَّهُ آدَمَ مِنْ حَدِّ ٱلتَّأْوِيلِ بِلَا حِجَابٍ، وَلَمْ يُخْلِهِ مِنَ ٱلْمَادَّةِ فَصَارَ مَا كَانَ يَتَّصِلُ بِهِ مِنَ ٱلْمَادَّةِ يَتَنَزَّلُ عَلَيْهِ مِنَ ٱلْحُدُودِ ٱلرُّوحَانِيَّةِ ٱلْعُلْوِيَّةِ، وَيَتَّصِلُ عَنْهُ بِٱلْحُدُودِ ٱلسُّفْلِيَّةِ، ذَٰلِكَ عَنْ آيَاتِ ٱللَّهِ كَمَا ذَكَرَهُ، وَجَرَىٰ ذَٰلِكَ كَذَٰلِكَ فِي ٱلنُّطَقَاءِ مِنْ وَلَدِهِ

 Allah (SWT) sent Adam down from the realm of interpretation without a veil, retaining his essence. Consequently, the substance connected to him began to descend from higher spiritual realms and link to lower realms. This reflects one of the signs of Allah (SWT), as He noted, and the same process occurred in the speakers from his lineage.

 

 قَالَ رَسُولُ ٱللَّهِ (صَلَّىٰ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَآلِهِ وَسَلَّمَ): إِنَّنِي آخُذُ ٱلْوَحْيَ عَنْ جِبْرِيلَ،

 The Messenger of Allah (Muhammad PBUH) said: "Indeed, I receive the revelation from Gabriel.

 

 وَجِبْرِيلُ يَأْخُذُهُ عَنْ مِيكَائِيلَ،

 And Gabriel receives it from Michael.

 

 وَمِيكَائِيلُ يَأْخُذُهُ عَنْ إِسْرَافِيلَ،

 And Michael receives it from Israfil.

 

 وَإِسْرَافِيلُ يَأْخُذُهُ عَنِ ٱللَّوْحِ،

 And Israfil receives it from the Tablet.

 

 وَٱللَّوْحُ يَأْخُذُهُ عَنِ ٱلْقَلَمِ،

 And the Tablet receives it from the Pen.

 

 فَصَارَ ٱلتَّأْيِيدُ يَتَّصِلُ بِٱلنُّطَقَاءِ مِنْ خَمْسَةِ حُدُودٍ عُلْوِيَّةٍ،

 So, the support connects to the speakers from five higher boundaries.

 

 وَيَتَّصِلُ عَنْهُمْ لِلْمُسْتَجِيبِينَ بِوَاسِطَةِ خَمْسَةِ حُدُودٍ سُفْلِيَّةٍ،

 And it connects them to the responders through five lower boundaries.

 

 فَٱلْحُدُودُ ٱلْعُلْوِيَّةُ هُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ ذَكَرْنَاهُمْ،

 The higher boundaries are those we have mentioned.

 

 وَٱلْحُدُودُ ٱلسُّفْلِيَّةُ هُمُ ٱلْأَمْسُ وَٱلْأَئِمَّةُ وَٱلْحُجَجُ وَٱلنُّقَبَاءُ أَصْحَابُ ٱلْجَزَائِرِ، وَٱلْأَجْنِحَةُ وَهُمُ ٱلدُّعَاةُ،

 The lower boundaries are the Ams (yesterday), the Imams, the Proofs, the Leaders (Nuqaba), the possessors of the islands, and the Wings, who are the callers.

 

 وَٱلسَّادِسُ مِنَ ٱلْحَدَّيْنِ هُوَ ٱلْقَابِلُ،

 

 And the sixth of the two boundaries is the one who accepts.

 وَصَارَ إِبْلِيسُ عَدُوًّا لِآدَمَ وَضِدَّهُ،

 

 And Iblis became an enemy to Adam and his opponent.

 وَكُلُّ مَنْ وَالَىٰ ٱللَّهَ تَعَالَىٰ مِنْ نَاطِقٍ وَصَامِتٍ وَإِمَامٍ وَحُجَّةٍ،

 

 And everyone who allies himself with Allah (SWT), from speaker and silent one, Imam and Proof.

 وَمَنْ تَوَلَّاهُمْ وَٱنْتَمَىٰ بِهِمْ فَهُوَ مِنْ حِزْبِ آدَمَ ٱلَّذِي هُوَ حِزْبُ ٱللَّهِ تَعَالَىٰ،

 

 And whoever takes them as allies and affiliates with them, then he is from the party of Adam, which is the party of Allah (SWT).

 وَكُلُّ مَنْ عَادَىٰ أَوْلِيَاءَ ٱللَّهِ وَنَاصَبَهُمْ، وَبَعُدَ عَنْ أَمْرِهِمْ أَوِ ٱدَّعَىٰ مَقَامَهُمْ، فَهُوَ مِنْ حِزْبِ إِبْلِيسَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱسْتَحْوَذَ عَلَيْهِمُ ٱلشَّيْطَانُ فَأَنْسَاهُمْ ذِكْرَ ٱللَّهِ،

 

 And everyone who opposes the close servants of Allah (SWT) and contends with them and distances himself from their command or claims their station, then he is from the party of Iblis, those whom Satan has overcome and made them forget the remembrance of Allah (SWT).

 وَٱلنِّسْيَانُ هَهُنَا هُوَ ٱلتَّأْخِيرُ بِقَوْلِهِ أَخَّرَهُمْ عَنْ ذِكْرِ ٱللَّهِ،

 

 And the forgetting here is the delaying, by His saying, "He delayed them from the remembrance of Allah (SWT)."

 وَذِكْرُ ٱللَّهِ هَهُنَا فِي ٱلْبَاطِنِ هُمْ أَوْلِيَاؤُهُ ٱلَّذِينَ بِهِمْ يُذْكَرُ، وَهُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ أَتَوْا بِذِكْرِهِ،

 

 And the remembrance of Allah (SWT) here in the inner meaning are His close servants by whom He is remembered, and they are the ones who brought His remembrance.

 وَجَرَىٰ مِثْلُ ذَٰلِكَ مَا كَانَ مِنْ آدَمَ وَحَوَّاءَ فِي ذُرِّيَّتِهَا،

 

 And similarly occurred what was from Adam and Eve in their offspring.

 وَذُرِّيَّتُهَا وَإِنْ كَثُرَتْ لَا تَعْدُو جِنْسَيْهَا مِنْ ذَكَرٍ وَأُنْثَىٰ كَمَا كَانَ ذَٰلِكَ أَعْنِي آدَمَ وَحَوَّاءَ

 

 And their offspring, even if they multiplied, do not exceed their two genders of male and female, just as that was, meaning Adam and Eve.

 وَكَذَٰلِكَ جَرَىٰ فِي جَمِيعِهِمْ مِنَ ٱلطَّاعَةِ وَٱلْمَعْصِيَةِ مِثْلُمَا جَرَىٰ فِي آدَمَ وَحَوَّاءَ،

 

 And likewise proceeded in all of them of obedience and disobedience just as proceeded in Adam and Eve.

 وَٱبْتِدَاءُ آدَمَ فِي خَلْقِهِ وَمَا جَرَىٰ عَلَيْهِ، وَذَٰلِكَ نَظِيرُ مَا يَجْرِي عَلَىٰ ٱلْخَلْقِ مِنْ وَلَدِهِ فِي ٱبْتِدَاءِ أَمْرِهِ، وَكَوْنِهِ فِي هَٰذِهِ ٱلدَّارِ،

 

 And the beginning of Adam in his creation and what befell him, that is similar to what befalls the creation from his offspring in the beginning of their matter and their being in this abode.

 وَإِسْكَانُ ٱللَّهِ سُبْحَانَهُ آدَمَ وَحَوَّاءَ ٱلْجَنَّةَ بَعْدَ مَا جَرَىٰ عَلَيْهَا، نَظِيرُ إِسْكَانِ ٱللَّهِ تَعَالَىٰ إِيَّاهَا مَنْ آمَنَ وَصَلُحَ مِنْ ذُرِّيَّتِهِمَا فَمَنْ هُوَ مِنْ حِزْبِهِمَا،

 

 And Allah (SWT) the Glorified settling Adam and Eve in Paradise after what befell them is similar to Allah (SWT) settling therein whoever believed and was righteous from their offspring, whoever is from their party.

 وَتَوْبَةُ مَنْ تَابَ مِنْهُمْ كَتَوْبَتِهِمَا،

 

 And the repentance of whoever repents from them is like their repentance.

 وَهَلَاكُ مَنْ هَلَكَ مِنْهُمْ بِمَعْصِيَةٍ، وَصَارَ إِلَىٰ عَذَابِ ٱللَّهِ بِكُفْرِهِ وَٱرْتِكَابِهِ مَا نَهَىٰ عَنْهُ جَلَّ ذِكْرُهُ، لِٱسْتِحْوَاذِ إِبْلِيسَ عَلَيْهِمْ وَكَوْنِهِمْ مِنْ حِزْبِهِ، وَٱنْقِطَاعِهِمْ عَنْ آدَمَ، وَخُرُوجِهِمْ عَنْ نِسْبَتِهِ، فَدَخَلُوا مَدْخَلَ مَنْ صَارُوا مِنْهُ، وَبَانُوا عَنْهُ، أَيْ مِنْ آدَمَ لِٱنْقِطَاعِهِمْ عَنْهُ

 

 And the destruction of whoever is destroyed from them by disobedience and goes to the punishment of Allah (SWT) by his disbelief and his committing what He forbade, whose mention is majestic, because of the overcoming of Iblis over them and their being from his party and their separation from Adam and their departure from his lineage, so they entered the entrance of those from whom they became and separated from him, meaning from Adam because of their separation from him.

 قَالَ ٱللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ: ﴿وَمَنْ يَتَوَلَّهُمْ مِنْكُمْ فَإِنَّهُ مِنْهُمْ﴾

 

 Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, said: "And whoever is an ally to them among you - then indeed, he is [one] of them."

 

 وَمَنْ تَوَلَّىٰ إِبْلِيسَ فَهُوَ مِنْهُ، وَهُمْ وَلَدُهُ بِٱلدَّعْوَةِ إِلَيْهِ، وَأَهْلُهُ بِٱلِٱتِّصَالِ بِهِ، وَبَانُوا مِنْ آدَمَ لِقَوْلِ ٱللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فِي ٱبْنِ نُوحٍ لَمَّا عَصَاهُ: ﴿إِنَّهُ لَيْسَ مِنْ أَهْلِكَ﴾

 

 And whoever allies himself with Iblis, then he is of him, and they are his children by the call to him, and his family by connection with him, and they have separated from Adam because of the saying of Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, about the son of Noah when he disobeyed him: "Indeed, he is not of your family."

 وَتَسْمِيَتُهُ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَىٰ حِزْبَ ٱلشَّيْطَانِ وَسَمَّاهُمْ بِٱسْمِهِ، وَقَالَ تَعَالَىٰ: ﴿إِنَّ الشَّيْطَانَ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّ مُبِينٌ﴾ وَقَالَ: ﴿إِنَّ عِبَادِي لَيْسَ لَكَ عَلَيْهِمْ سُلْطَانٌ﴾ يَعْنِي ٱتِّبَاعَ آدَمَ مِنْ وَلَدِهِ ﴿إِلَّا مَنِ اتَّبَعَكَ مِنَ الْغَاوِينَ وَإِنَّ جَهَنَّمَ لَمَوْعِدُهُمْ أَجْمَعِينَ لَهَا سَبْعَةُ أَبْوَابٍ لِكُلِّ بَابٍ مِنْهُمْ جُزْءٌ مَقْسُومٌ﴾

 

 And His naming, the Glorified and Exalted, the party of Satan, and He named them by his name, and the Exalted said: "Indeed, Satan is to you a clear enemy." And He said: "Indeed, My servants - no authority will you have over them," meaning the followers of Adam from his offspring, "except whoever follows you of the deviators. And indeed, Hell is their promised place, all of them. It has seven gates; for every gate of them is a portion designated."

 وَيَعْنِي بِٱلْأَبْوَابِ ٱلرُّسُلَ ٱلَّذِينَ هُمْ أَبْوَابُ ٱلظَّاهِرِ ٱلْمَحْضِ لِأَنَّهُ لِكُلِّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمْ شَرِيعَةٌ مَعْلُومَةٌ، وَقِسْمٌ مَنْ دَفَعَهَا يُوَلُّوا حُبَّهُمُ ٱلنَّارَ وَمِنْهَا قَوْلُ رَسُولِ ٱللَّهِ (صَلَّىٰ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَآلِهِ وَسَلَّمَ) لِعَلِيٍّ: «وَأَنْتَ قَيِّمُ ٱلنَّارِ»، لِذَٰلِكَ يَقُولُ لَهَا يَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَامَةِ: «وَهَٰذَا لَكِ فَخُذِيهِ، وَهَٰذَا لِي فَٱتْرُكِيهِ».

 

 And by the gates He means the messengers who are the gates of the pure apparent meaning, because each one of them has a known law, and a portion of those who reject it will turn their love towards the Fire. And from that is the saying of the Messenger of Allah (Muhammad PBUH) to Ali: "And you are the overseer of the Fire." For that reason, he will say to it on the Day of Resurrection: "This is for you, so take it, and this is for me, so leave it."

 وَمَعْنَىٰ هَٰذَا أَنَّ مُضَادَّةَ إِبْلِيسَ لِآدَمَ وَعَدَاوَتَهُ، كَمُضَادَّةِ أَعْدَاءِ ٱلرُّسُلِ إِيَّاهُمْ، وَعَدَاوَتِهِمْ لَهُمْ، قَالَ ٱللَّهُ تَعَالَىٰ: ﴿وَكَذَلِكَ جَعَلْنَا لِكُلِّ نَبِيٍّ عَدُوًّا مِنَ الْمُجْرِمِينَ﴾

 

 And the meaning of this is that Iblis' opposition to Adam and his enmity towards him are like the opposition of the enemies of the messengers to them and their enmity towards them. Allah (SWT) the Exalted said: "And thus We have made for every prophet an enemy among the criminals."

 فَٱلْمُذْنِبُ مِنْ وَلَدِ آدَمَ، ٱلْمُصِرُّ عَلَىٰ ذَنْبِهِ، ٱلَّذِي لَا يَتُوبُ مِنْهُ، وَلَا يُقْلِعُ عَنْهُ.

 

 So the sinner from the children of Adam, who persists in his sin, who does not repent from it, and does not desist from it.

 فَمَثَلُهُ مَثَلُ إِبْلِيسَ فِي إِصْرَارِهِ، وَقَدْ خَرَجَ مِنْ حِزْبِ آدَمَ، وَدَخَلَ فِي حِزْبِهِ، وَدَخَلَ مَدْخَلَهُ، وَصَارَ إِلَىٰ عَذَابِ ٱللَّهِ مَعَهُ، أَيْ مَعَ إِبْلِيسَ،

 

 His likeness is the likeness of Iblis in his persistence, and he has left the party of Adam and entered his party and entered his entrance and gone to the punishment of Allah (SWT) with him, meaning with Iblis.

 وَٱلتَّائِبُ ٱلْمُقْلِعُ عَمَّا ٱقْتَرَفَ، وَٱلْمُطِيعُ فَهُوَ مِنْ وَلَدِ آدَمَ وَحِزْبِهِ ٱلَّذِي هُوَ حِزْبُ ٱللَّهِ جَلَّ ذِكْرُهُ.

 

 And the repentant who desists from what he has committed, and the obedient, then he is from the children of Adam and his party which is the party of Allah (SWT), whose mention is majestic.

 قَالَ ٱللَّهُ تَعَالَىٰ: ﴿وَالَّذِينَ إِذَا فَعَلُوا فَاحِشَةً أَوْ ظَلَمُوا أَنْفُسَهُمْ ذَكَرُوا اللَّهَ فَاسْتَغْفَرُوا لِذُنُوبِهِمْ وَمَنْ يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ وَلَمْ يُصِرُّوا عَلَى مَا فَعَلُوا وَهُمْ يَعْلَمُونَ أُولَئِكَ جَزَاؤُهُمْ مَغْفِرَةٌ مِنْ رَبِّهِمْ وَجَنَّاتٌ تَجْرِي مِنْ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ خَالِدِينَ فِيهَا وَنِعْمَ أَجْرُ الْعَامِلِينَ﴾

 

 Allah (SWT) the Exalted said: "And those who, when they commit an immorality or wrong themselves [by transgression], remember Allah and seek forgiveness for their sins - and who can forgive sins except Allah? - and [who] do not persist in what they have done while they know. Those - their reward is forgiveness from their Lord and gardens beneath which rivers flow, wherein they will abide eternally; and excellent is the reward of the [righteous] workers."

 فَأَيَّدَ ٱللَّهُ سُبْحَانَهُ آدَمَ بِٱلْوَصِيَّةِ، وَأَسْكَنَهُ ٱلْأَرْضَ، وَٱسْتَخْلَفَهُ عَلَىٰ مَنْ فِيهَا، وَتَنَاسَلَتْ ذُرِّيَّتُهُ بِهَا، وَكَانَ حُجَّةَ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِمْ، وَٱلنَّاطِقَ بِٱلرِّسَالَةِ إِلَيْهِمْ، وَأَوْصَىٰ إِلَىٰ ٱبْنِهِ هَابِيلَ فَحَسَدَهُ أَخُوهُ قَابِيلُ وَقَتَلَهُ، كَمَا وَصَفَ ٱللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ.

 

 So Allah (SWT) the Glorified supported Adam with the testament, and settled him on the earth, and made him a vicegerent over those therein, and his offspring multiplied therein, and he was the proof of Allah (SWT) over them, and the speaker of the message to them, and he bequeathed to his son Abel, but his brother Cain envied him and killed him, as Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, described.

 وَذَٰلِكَ فِي حَيَاةِ آدَمَ لِتَصِيرَ ٱلْوَصِيَّةُ إِلَيْهِ، فِيمَا تَوَهَّمَهُ وَظَنَّهُ، وَمَضَىٰ فِي عِصْيَانِهِ، وَأَصَرَّ عَلَىٰ ذَنْبِهِ، وَكَانَ مِنْ حِزْبِ إِبْلِيسَ.

 

 And that was in the lifetime of Adam, so that the testament would go to him, in what he imagined and thought, and he persisted in his disobedience and insisted on his sin, and he was of the party of Iblis.

 وَوَهَبَ ٱللَّهُ لِآدَمَ مِنْ ذُرِّيَّتِهِ شِيثًا، فَسَمَّاهُ (هِبَةَ ٱللَّهِ)، وَأَوْدَعَهُ ٱلْعِلْمَ، وَأَسَرَّهُ إِلَيْهِ، وَأَمَرَهُ بِكِتْمَانِهِ، تَقِيَّةً عَلَيْهِ مِنْ قَابِيلَ أَنْ يَقْتُلَهُ، كَمَا قَتَلَ هَابِيلَ، فَجَرَتْ بِذَٰلِكَ ٱلسُّنَّةُ فِي أَسْرَارِ ٱلْعَهْدِ وَحِفْظِهِ،

 

 And Allah (SWT) bestowed upon Adam from his offspring Seth, so he named him "Gift of Allah," and entrusted him with knowledge and confided in him and commanded him to conceal it, as a precaution for him against Cain killing him as he had killed Abel, so the way was thus established in the secrets of the covenant and its preservation.

 وَقَيَّضَ ٱللَّهُ آدَمَ إِلَىٰ مَا أَعَدَّهُ لَهُ مِنَ ٱلثَّوَابِ فِي دَارِ ٱلْقَرَارِ، وَقَامَ بِأَمْرِ ٱلْإِمَامَةِ مِنْ بَعْدِهِ وَصِيُّهُ شِيثٌ، وَجَرَتِ ٱلْأُمُورُ فِي وَلَدِهِ عَلَىٰ حَسَبِ مَا قَدَّمْنَا ذِكْرَهُ مِنْ قَائِمٍ، بَعْدَ قَائِمٍ، وَإِمَامٍ بَعْدَ إِمَامٍ

 

 And Allah (SWT) destined Adam to what He had prepared for him of reward in the abode of permanence, and his successor Seth took charge of the matter of Imamate after him, and affairs proceeded in his offspring according to what we have previously mentioned of a قائم (one who stands) after a قائم, and an Imam after an Imam.

 وَكَانَ مِمَّنْ ذَكَرَ ٱللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فِي كِتَابِهِ عَنْ إِدْرِيسَ عَلَيْهِ ٱلسَّلَامُ، وَهُوَ مِنْ دَوْرِ آدَمَ صَلَوَاتُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ، ثُمَّ ٱنْتَهَىٰ ٱلْأَمْرُ إِلَىٰ نُوحٍ صَلَوَاتُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ، فَبَعَثَهُ ٱللَّهُ سُبْحَانَهُ رَسُولًا نَاطِقًا بِشَرِيعَةٍ غَيْرِ شَرِيعَةِ آدَمَ، قَالَ ﴿لِكُلِّ أُمَّةٍ جَعَلْنَا مِنْهُمْ شِرْعَةً وَمِنْهَاجًا﴾ وَٱنْقَضَىٰ دَوْرُ آدَمَ بِقِيَامِ نُوحٍ صَلَوَاتُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْهِمْ

 

 And among those whom Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, mentioned in His Book was Idris (peace be upon him), and he was from the era of Adam (peace be upon him). Then the matter ended with Noah (peace be upon him), so Allah (SWT) the Glorified sent him as a speaking messenger with a law other than the law of Adam. He said: "For every nation We have appointed a law and a method." And the era of Adam ended with the rise of Noah (peace be upon them).

 وَكُلٌّ مِنْهُمْ لَمْ يَقُمْ بِٱلرِّسَالَةِ إِلَّا بَعْدَ أَنْ كَانَ مُتَمَسِّكًا بِشَرِيعَةِ مَنْ كَانَ قَبْلَهُ وَقَدْ أُخِذَ عَلَيْهِ مِيثَاقُهَا وَكَانَ مُتَمَسِّكًا بِهَا، قَالَ ٱللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ: ﴿وَإِذْ أَخَذْنَا مِنَ النَّبِيِّينَ مِيثَاقَهُمْ وَمِنْكَ وَمِنْ نُوحٍ وَإِبْرَاهِيمَ وَمُوسَى وَعِيسَى ابْنِ مَرْيَمَ وَأَخَذْنَا مِنْهُمْ مِيثَاقًا غَلِيظًا﴾

 

 وَقَدْ ذَكَرْنَا ذَٰلِكَ فِيمَا تَقَدَّمَ،

 

 And we have mentioned that previously.

 فَٱفْهَمُوا مَعْشَرَ ٱلْمُؤْمِنِينَ مَا تَلَوْنَاهُ عَلَيْكُمْ مِنْ تَأْوِيلِ مَا أَنْزَلَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْكُمْ،

 

 So understand, O believers, what we have recited to you of the interpretation of what Allah (SWT) has revealed to you.

 وَٱعْرِفُوا حَقِيقَةَ مَا بِهِ تَعَبَّدَكُمْ وَبَاطِنَ ٱلتَّنْزِيلِ ٱلَّذِي أَخْبَرَكُمْ عَنْهُ سُبْحَانَهُ فِي كِتَابِهِ أَنَّهُ لَا يَعْلَمُهُ إِلَّا هُوَ وَٱلرَّاسِخُونَ فِي ٱلْعِلْمِ مِنْ أَوْلِيَائِهِ ٱلَّذِينَ أَنْعَمَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْكُمْ بِوَلَايَتِهِمْ وَٱتِّبَاعِهِمْ فَجَعَلَكُمْ بِذَٰلِكَ مِنْ حِزْبِهِ ٱلَّذِي وَعَدَهُ بِثَوَابِهِ،

 

 And know the reality of what He has made you worship Him with, and the inner meaning of the revelation about which the Glorified informed you in His Book that none knows it except Him and those firm in knowledge from His close servants whom Allah (SWT) has favored you with their guardianship and following them, so He has made you thereby from His party which He has promised with His reward.

 وَٱسْتَنْقَذَكُمْ بِهِ مِنْ حِزْبِ إِبْلِيسَ ٱلَّذِي تَوَعَّدَهُ عِقَابَهُ

 

 And He has saved you by it from the party of Iblis whom He has threatened with His punishment.

 وَٱعْمَلُوا بِمَا ٱفْتَرَضَ ٱللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْعَمَلَ بِهِ،

 

 And act upon what Allah (SWT), the Mighty and Majestic, has made obligatory for you to act upon.

 وَأَخْلِصُوا لِوَجْهِهِ،

 

 And be sincere for His sake.

 وَقُومُوا ظَاهِرًا وَبَاطِنًا بِمَا ٱفْتَرَضَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْكُمْ،

 

 And fulfill outwardly and inwardly what Allah (SWT) has made obligatory for you.

 وَمَنِ ٱقْتَرَفَ مِنْكُمْ ذَنْبًا فَلْيَتُبْ إِلَىٰ ٱللَّهِ مِنْهُ،

 

 And whoever among you has committed a sin, let him repent to Allah (SWT) from it.

 وَلْيَتَلَقَّ فِيهِ وَلِيَّ زَمَانِهِ لِيَسْتَغْفِرَ ٱللَّهَ لَهُ،

 

 And let him seek the Imam of his time in that, so that he may ask Allah (SWT) for forgiveness for him.

 وَيَسْتَغْفِرَ هُوَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ،

 

 And he may seek forgiveness for his sin.

 وَيَصِيرَ إِلَىٰ مَا وَعَدَ ٱللَّهُ ٱلتَّائِبِينَ بِهِ، ٱلَّذِينَ ٱسْتَغْفَرُوا لِذُنُوبِهِمْ، وَٱسْتَغْفَرَ أَوْلِيَاؤُهُ لَهُمْ لِتَكُونُوا مِنْ حِزْبِ أَبِيكُمْ آدَمَ، وَحِزْبِ أَوْلِيَاءِ ٱللَّهِ مِنْ وَلَدِهِ ٱلَّذِي هُوَ حِزْبُ ٱللَّهِ جَلَّ ذِكْرُهُ،

 

 And return to what Allah (SWT) has promised the repentant, those who sought forgiveness for their sins, and whose close servants sought forgiveness for them, so that you may be from the party of your father Adam, and the party of the close servants of Allah (SWT) from his offspring, which is the party of Allah (SWT), whose mention is majestic.

 وَٱتْرُكُوا حِزْبَ ٱلشَّيْطَانِ ٱلَّذِينَ أَصَرُّوا عَلَىٰ ذُنُوبِهِمْ، كَمَا أَصَرَّ هُوَ عَلَىٰ ذَنْبِهِ، وَأَبْلَسُوا مِنْهُ كَمَا أَبْلَسَ هُوَ، كَمَا أَيِسَ مِنْ رَبِّهِ،

 

 And leave the party of Satan, those who persisted in their sins, just as he persisted in his sin, and despaired of it as he despaired, as he lost hope in his Lord.

 وَلَا تَيْأَسُوا مِنْ رَبِّكُمْ وَرَحْمَتِهِ إِيَّاكُمْ، فَقَدْ قَالَ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَىٰ: ﴿وَلَا تَيْأَسُوا مِنْ رَوْحِ اللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَا يَيْأَسُ مِنْ رَوْحِ اللَّهِ إِلَّا الْقَوْمُ الْكَافِرُونَ﴾

 

 And do not despair of your Lord and His mercy towards you, for the Glorified and Exalted said: "And do not despair of relief from Allah. Indeed, no one despairs of relief from Allah except the disbelieving people."

 وَكَمَا حَكَىٰ مِنْ قَوْلِهِ تَعَالَىٰ لِلْخَلِيلِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ: ﴿وَمَنْ يَقْنَطُ مِنْ رَحْمَةِ رَبِّهِ إِلَّا الضَّالُّونَ﴾

 

 And as He narrated from His saying, the Exalted, to His close friend Ibrahim: "And who despairs of the mercy of his Lord except for those astray?"

 وَفَّقَكُمُ ٱللَّهُ لِمَا يُحِبُّهُ وَيَرْضَاهُ،

 

 May Allah (SWT) grant you success in what He loves and is pleased with.

 وَوَقَاكُمْ مِنِ ٱرْتِكَابِ نَهْيِهِ وَمَعَاصِيهِ،

 

 And protect you from committing His prohibitions and acts of disobedience.

 وَفَتَحَ لَكُمْ أَبْوَابَ طَاعَةِ أَوْلِيَائِهِ،

 

 And open for you the doors of obedience to His close servants.

 وَصَلَّى ٱللَّهُ عَلَىٰ مُحَمَّدٍ وَآلِهِ، وَعَلَىٰ ٱلْأَئِمَّةِ ٱلطَّاهِرِينَ مِنْ عِتْرَتِهِ، وَسَلَّمَ تَسْلِيمًا كَثِيرًا.

 

 And may Allah (SWT) send blessings upon Muhammad and his family and upon the pure Imams from his progeny and grant them abundant peace.

 

 

 1) الادوار في المعتقدات الاسماعيلية تقسم إلى قسمين أدوار كبيرة وادوار صغيرة ، فالادوار الكبيرة هي التي تبتدي بآدم وتنتهي بالقيامة ، أما الادوار الصغيرة فهي التي تبتديء بقيام الناطق اي التي المرسل بشريعة وتنتهي بقيام الناطق الذي يليه . والدور الكبير الذي نحن فيه الآن مقسم إلى سبعة أدوار صغيرة وهم : دور آدم و نوح و ابراهيم وموسى وعيسى ومحمد والقائم المنتظر وأن لكل دور امام مقيم وامام متم وأساس وناطق وسبعة ائمة ، ومعنى المقيم هو الذي يربي الناطق في دعوته والمتم هو الذي يحتم الدور والاساس هو حجة الناطق في حياته ويتسلم الوصية بعده وهو صاحب التأويل الباطني كما ان الناطق صاحب العلم الشرعي الظاهري 

 لقد كان الامام المقيم في عهد آدم ، هنيد، وكان حججه اثني عشر ومنهم ابليس الذي رفض الاذعان لآدم ، أما آدم فقد هبط في عدن وربما كانت عدن الموجودة في اليمن ، ثم هاجر إلى مكة المكرمة وكان اسمها ( فاران ) وتوفي فيها وكان عمره /۹۳/ عاماً ودفن في سفح جبل أبي قبيس ، بعد أن أوصى الى ولده ( شيت ) وكان يسمى ( هبة الله ) . ولد شيث عام / ٢٣٠ / من هبوط آدم وتوفي عام /١١٤٢) وقد عاش / ۹۱٢) وقد أوصى لولده ( انوش ) ولد عام ٤٣٥/ من هبوط آدم وتوفي عام / ١٣٨٥ / وقد عاش / ١٩٥٠ عاماً بعد ان اوصى لولده ( قينان ) ، وقيتان ولد عام / ٦٢٥/من هبوط آدم وتوفي عام ١٥٣٥/ وقد عاش / ۹۱۰/ اعوام بعد أن أوصى لولده ( مهلئيل ) . و ( مهلئيل ) ولد عام ٧٩٥ وتوفي عام ١٩٩٠ من هبوط آدم بعد ان اوصى لولده يارد وقد عاش ٨٩٥ عام، وبارد ولد يعام ٩٦٠ وتوفي عام ۱۹۲۲ من هبوط آدم وقد عاش ٩٦٢ ثم أوصى لولده ( اخنوخ ) . واخنوخ ولد عام ۱۱۲۲ وقام عام ١٤٨٧ وقد عاش ٣٦٥ بعد ان اومی لولده ( متو شالح ) وكان اسمه ايضاً ادريس وسموه هرمس المثلث وهو أول من خط بالقلم . ولد متوشالح عام ۱۳٨٧ وتوفي عام ٢٢٤٢ وهو عام الطوفان وكانت وفاته قبيل الطوفان بقليل وعاش ٩٥٥ عام عام وأوصى لولده (1) ( لملك ) ولملك ولد عام ١٤٥٤ من هبوط آدم وتوفي عام ٢٣٤٦ اي بعد الطوفان ب ١٠٤ أعوام وعاش ۸۹۱ وهو متم دور آدم وسابع الأئمة وقد سلم الأمر الى ولده نوح بعد أن قضت الارادة الالهية بتغيير الشريعة القديمة والاستعاضة عنها بشريعة جديدة هي

  

 

 In Ismaili beliefs, historical periods, called 'Adwar', are divided into two types: major and minor. A major period begins with Adam and continues until the Day of Resurrection. A minor period starts with a 'Speaking Prophet' (Natiq), who brings a divine law, and ends with the next Speaking Prophet.

 The current major period we are in is made up of seven minor periods. These are the periods of Adam, Noah, Abraham, Moses, Jesus, Muhammad, and the awaited Qaim. In each minor period, there is an Abiding Imam (Imam Muqim), a Completing Imam (Imam Mutamm), a Foundation (Asas), a Speaking Prophet (Natiq), and seven Imams.

 The Abiding Imam is the one who nurtures the Speaking Prophet in his mission. The Completing Imam is the one who brings that period to a close. The Foundation is the Speaking Prophet's proof during his lifetime and inherits his authority afterwards. The Foundation holds the inner, esoteric interpretation, just as the Speaking Prophet holds the outward, exoteric religious law.

 In Adam's period, the Abiding Imam was Hunayd. His proofs included twelve individuals, among them Iblis, who refused to submit to Adam. Adam himself descended to Aden, possibly the Aden located in Yemen. He later migrated to Mecca, which was then called Faran, and passed away there at the age of 930. He was buried at the foot of Mount Abu Qubais. He designated his son Seth, also known as Hibbat Allah, as his successor.

 Seth was born 230 years after Adam's descent and lived for 912 years, passing away in year 1142. He appointed his son Enosh as his successor. Enosh was born 435 years after Adam's descent and lived for 950 years, dying in year 1385. He was succeeded by his son Kenan. Kenan was born 625 years after Adam's descent, lived for 910 years, and died in year 1535. He passed on his authority to his son Mahalalel. Mahalalel was born 795 years after Adam's descent, lived for 895 years, and died in year 1690. He designated his son Jared as his successor. Jared was born 960 years after Adam's descent, lived for 962 years, and died in year 1922. He then appointed his son Enoch as his successor.

 Enoch was born 1122 years after Adam's descent. His mission began in year 1487, and he lived for 365 years. He was also known as Idris and was called Hermes Trismegistus, being the first to write with a pen. Enoch was succeeded by his son Methuselah. Methuselah was born 1387 years after Adam's descent and died in year 2242, which was the year of the great flood, shortly before it occurred. He lived for 855 years and appointed his son Lamech as his successor.

 Lamech was born 1454 years after Adam's descent and died in year 2346, which was 104 years after the flood. He lived for 891 years. Lamech was the Completing Imam of Adam's period and the seventh Imam in that line. He handed over the authority to his son Noah, as divine will decreed a change in the old religious law and its replacement with a new one.

 

 Islam (Submission to Allah) and Iman (Faith) are the foundation for understanding the main rules, or "pillars," of Islam. These pillars are things Allah wants us to do, and doing them shows our obedience to Him. These are ولایۃ، طھارۃ، صلوۃ، صوم، زکوۃ، حج، جھاد . 

 Following Islam, there is obedience to Allah, His Messenger (Prophet Muhammad PBUH), and "those in authority among us." This comes from the Holy Quran:

 

 Women  [4:59] 

 [ سورۃ ۴]  النسا 

 ‏يَٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ آمَنُوٓا۟ أَطِيعُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَأَطِيعُوا۟ ٱلرَّسُولَ وَأُو۟لِى ٱلْأَمْرِ مِنكُمْ ۖ فَإِن تَنَزَعْتُمْ فِى شَىْءٍۢ فَرُدُّوهُ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ وَٱلرَّسُولِ إِن كُنتُمْ تُؤْمِنُونَ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْآخِرِ ۚ ذَلِكَ خَيْرٌۭ وَأَحْسَنُ تَأْوِيلًا ‎ ۵۹ 

 O you who believe! Obey Allah and obey the Messenger, and those of you who are in authority.  if you differ in anything amongst yourselves, refer it to Allah and His Messenger, if you believe in Allah and in the Last Day. That is better and more suitable for final determination. 

 So, while Islam involves submitting to and obeying Allah's commands, Iman encompasses submitting to and obeying the hierarchy that Allah has established based on the principle of لا الہ الا اللہ . These are the rightful prophets and Imams He has appointed. Even though these leaders may have different ranks or levels, Allah unites them in this command of obedience: أَطِيعُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَأَطِيعُوا۟ ٱلرَّسُولَ وَأُو۟لِى ٱلْأَمْرِ مِنكُمْ . 

 

 Spiritual Ranks: Speakers, Foundations, Imams, and Proofs 

 I learn about a kind of spiritual ranking:

 The Speakers (ٱلنُّطَقَاءُ - An-Nutuqa’) : These are at the highest level. They are major prophets who brought new divine laws or messages: Adam, Noah, Abraham, Moses, Jesus, and Muhammad (PBUH). The seventh one mentioned is the قائم ٱلزَّمَانِ (Qa’im az-Zaman), the Establisher or Master of the Age. Think of them like the main messengers bringing big updates for humanity.

 The Foundations (ٱلْأُسُسُ - Al-Usus) : After a Speaker, comes a Foundation. The Speaker chooses this person during his lifetime to explain the more profound, hidden meanings (called batin - بَاطِن) of the message.

 The Imams (ٱلْأَئِمَّةِ - Al-A’immah) : The Foundation becomes the Imam (leader) after the Speaker passes away. The Imam guides the community.

 The Proofs (حُجَّة - Hujjah) : The Imam then appoints a Proof (Hujjah) who will eventually take the Imam's place.

 This chain continues like a cycle. The earth is never, ever empty, not even for a blink of an eye, without someone representing Allah's proof. Eventually, one cycle ends, and a new Speaker comes with a new law, starting the cycle again. This system ensures that complete spiritual excellence is not in two people at once but passes from one leader to the next, reflecting Allah's Oneness. Allah says:

 

 The Prophets  [21:22] 

 [ سورۃ ۲۱]  الانبیائ 

 ‏لَوْ كَانَ فِيهِمَآ آلِهَةٌ إِلَّا ٱللَّهُ لَفَسَدَتَا ۚ فَسُبْحَنَ ٱللَّهِ رَبِّ ٱلْعَرْشِ عَمَّا يَصِفُونَ ‎ ۲۲ 

 Had there been within the heavens and earth gods besides Allah , they both would have been ruined. So exalted is Allah , Lord of the Throne, above what they describe.

 This means if there were other gods besides Allah, the heavens and earth would be ruined. So, having one leader at a time reflects this unity.

 

 Speaker (Natiq) and Silent One (Samit) 

 The leader of the time (whether a Prophet, Messenger, or Imam) is called the Speaker (نَاطِق - Natiq) because they speak and establish the apparent or outer rules (called zahir - ظَاهِر). Their Proof (Hujjah) is called the Silent One (صَامِت - Samit) because they are silent about the outer rules but are the keeper of the inner, hidden knowledge ( batin - بَاطِن).

 This relationship is compared to the cycle of day and night:

 The Imam (Speaker/Natiq) is like the Day : bringing the clear, apparent light and guidance.

 The Hujjah (Silent/Samit) is like the Night : concealing and protecting the deep, inner knowledge.

 Allah says in the Quran:

 Q|17:12

 These signs, in their inner meaning, are Allah's Proofs, the Imams we must obey and follow.

 The Silent One (Samit/Hujjah) is also connected to the twelve successors (ٱللَّوَاحِقِ ٱلِٱثْنَيْ عَشَرَ - al-Lawahiq al-Ithnay ‘Ashar). This reminds me of when Allah mentioned choosing twelve leaders (نَقِيبًا - Naqiban) in the Quran:

 Q|5:12

 Imam and Natiq: Interchangeable Titles 

 The title "Imam" means leader, someone people follow, like the person leading prayers. The title "Speaker" (Natiq) applies to the Imam because they speak the same message the original Speaking Messenger spoke, especially the outer law. So, these titles can apply to the same person. The example of Prophet Abraham being made an Imam is used:

 Q|2:124

 Here, Allah tells Abraham He will make him an Imam (leader) for people.

 Sometimes, leaders might invite people to the wrong path, and the Quran calls them "leaders inviting to the Fire":

 Q|28:41

## Session 6: The Timelines

*Source: KSessions Group 18, Session ID 1282, 3272 words*

Allah - Name or Title?

 If Allah is a name, who named him? Naming is done by someone superior. Throughout history, various names have been used to call him in different languages. 

 Aramaic: The language spoken during the time of Jesus, he was called ʼĔlāhā  

 Syriac: A dialect of Aramaic. He was called Alāhā (ܐܰܠܳܗܳܐ), 

 Hebrew: The language of the Torah. He has been called El (אֵל): A generic term for "god" or "God."

 Eloah (אֱלוֹהַּ): A singular form for "God," directly related to Aramaic Elaha and Arabic Allah .

 Elohim (אֱלֹהִים): A plural form grammatically, but often used with a singular meaning to refer to the one God in the Hebrew Bible. It shares the same root.

 

 Akkadian: He was called Ilu .

 Sanskrit Scriptures: He was called Īśvara (ईश्वर) and Parameśvara (परमेश्वर) - the supreme Being.

 Islam: He is called Allah.

 Various names have been used across different religions to refer to God throughout history, but they all convey the same essence.

 The one who bewilders the mind

 The one Supreme Being who is truly worthy of submission, worship, and obedience.

 Definition Of Allah

 

 Ahad : Must be unique, meaning that during his reign, no one can match his capabilities or powers.

 Samad : Must be whole and self-sufficient, free from any deficiencies, flaws, or weaknesses.

 Transcendence:

 Lam Yalid (Did Not Beget) - He has no offspring, indicating there is no resemblance to His creation. The progeny of a cat is a cat, a dog’s offspring is a dog, and a man's is a child. Offspring share similarities in appearance, qualities, traits, and abilities with their father. While we reproduce and He created us, our nature is not comparable. Think about the difference between an artisan crafting a unique masterpiece through skill and intent, distinct from the artist himself, and a living being giving birth (which suggests shared nature and biological connection). Lam Yalid emphasizes that Allah's connection to creation mirrors the former. Despite His act of creation, He remains unique and separated from it, just as the artist remains entirely distinct from his sculpture. 

 Lam Yulad (Was not Begotten) - He was not created - meaning his existence did not require the fusion of sperm and egg, nor did he go through the seven stages of creation, like a fetus goes through in its mother's womb. 

 Kufu : He is incomparable in every possible sense of the word. No other being possesses his rank, his infallible knowledge, his authority, or his functions. In other words, he has no equal in his dominion.

 What does the combination of these attributes produce? An entity that bewilders. An entity that is worthy of submission and obedience. 

 If we accept that "Allah" is a title rather than a name, then metaphorically, anyone embodying the attributes described in Surah Ikhlas can be referred to as "Allah." This idea does not imply "Shirk" since Surah Ikhlas defines the concept in a way that rules out a partnership. It doesn't mean that we deny the existence of the one Supreme being who created the universe and everything within it. Certainly not. He remains the only deity deserving of our worship and prostration. My point is that the title "Allah" can apply to anyone who fulfills the definition of Surah Ikhlas in their context. This distinction will become clearer shortly. For now, can we all agree that Allah is a "title" and not a "name"? And anyone who meets this criterion can be called "Allah"? 

 The Imam as Allah

 Now, let's see if the definition of Surah al-Ikhlas can be applied esoterically to the Imam.

 "Qul Huwa Allahu Ahad" (Say: He is Allah, the One) 

 In every age, the rightful Imam is unique . He is احد because he is the source of infallible divine guidance and authentic تاویل . There is only one true Imam for all of humanity at any given time. His authority in interpreting the faith and guiding the community is singular and unparalleled within the human realm for that era. He represents the unity of divinely guided authority on Earth.

 "Allahu al-Samad" (Allah, the Eternal Refuge / Self-Sufficient) 

 The Imam serves as the spiritual refuge and support for the believers. We all turn to him for spiritual sustenance, knowledge ( علم ), interpretation ( تاویل ), and intercession. He is the focal point for the community's spiritual needs and the reliable source we turn to for guidance on the path to salvation. Therefore, he is Samad - completely self-sufficient with absolutely no weakness.

 "Lam yalid wa lam yulad" (He neither begets nor is born/begotten) 

 . However, the تاویل  relates to the spiritual reality   and authority   of the Imamate:
 

 Lam yūlad (spiritually "unbegotten" )  does not have a father.   What does this mean? Because we know the Imam is physically born ( یولد ) and has offspring ( یلد ), including his son, who becomes his successor. So, how does this not being born apply to the Imam?  

 The core idea is that while the Imam's physical existence begins at birth, the Divine Light ( نور الامامۃ ) , infallible knowledge ( علم ), and the spiritual authority he embodies are not derived from his physical birth or earthly sources. Instead, they represent a pre-existing, divinely ordained reality that emerges from the spiritual realms, ultimately rooted in God's Command ( Amr ).
Additionally, concerning physical birth, a child undergoes seven stages in the womb before being born into the world. From an esoteric perspective, the Imam does not progress through stages of learning to gain knowledge, wisdom, and authority. Instead, he receives these directly from Allah via a divine light emanating from Allah's command. Thus, in this regard, he lacks a father. Why? Typically, a father teaches his trade skills to his son, but the Imam does not learn from his father; Allah directly grants him knowledge. Thus, he exists without needing a father in this esoteric context. So the term  لم یولد applies to an Imam in that esoteric context.

 Analogy: Think of the Imam as the Moon in the night sky. The Moon itself is a physical body that exists in space, just like the Imam's physical body, which is born. However, the beautiful light the Moon shines does not originate from the Moon's substance; it is not "begotten" by the Moon itself. The light is a reflection of the Sun (representing the ultimate Divine Source or the higher spiritual emanations like the Universal Intellect).

 

 Lam yalid (spiritually "non-begetting" Does not have a son.   This relates to the concept I just discussed. The fundamental idea is that the Imam, serving as the focal point of Divine Light (نور) and Guidance (علم), does not produce or originate this spiritual essence from within himself, like how a parent begets a child who shares their nature, traits, and DNA. Instead, the Imam manifests, reflects, or channels the divine light he receives from the spiritual realm to the believers, similar to the moon analogy I mentioned earlier.

 Another analogy would be a mirror. A clean, perfect mirror accurately shows the image of whatever is before it. The image appears in   the mirror, but the mirror doesn't create or "beget" the object or its image. The infallible Imam, due to his purity and divine designation, perfectly reflects the Divine Will and Guidance for the people of his time. The truth appears through him, but he isn't the ultimate originator of that truth. He doesn't "beget" the divine reality that he reflects. 

 Lam Yalid here signifies that the mirror (Imam) doesn't generate the object (Divine Reality/Guidance) but rather reflects it for the believers.

 

 "Wa lam yakun lahu kufuwan ahad" (And there is none comparable/equivalent unto Him) In the hierarchy of creation and spiritual authority of his time, the Imam stands unmatched ( کفوا ). No other individual holds his status, infallible knowledge ( 'ilm ), his role as the Proof of God, or his unique position as the sole legitimate interpreter of the faith's more profound meaning. In his divinely appointed position, he had no peer among humanity during his time.

 

 Timelines

 Physical Day - 24 Hours

 

 Religious Day - 57,000 Years

 

 

 

 

 Where are we today?

 

 SITR - Nutaqa

 

 
 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 The Three Timeframes

 Angels

 As we discussed earlier, this blissful period of 47,000 years is called دور کشف . The word کشف  means "Reveal or Unveil,". The reason it's called کشف  is because the Imam is always present to guide believers, much like the sun, which graciously shines and constantly provides warmth and light. The sun is a symbol of the Imam.

 

 This remarkable era is characterized by happiness, tranquility, and peace. During these 47,000 years, there is no evil, just as darkness cannot exist when the sun shines. This is why this serene period is referred to as "paradise" ( جنۃ ) in many verses of the Quran. Several different types of جنۃ  exist; this is merely one example. Similarly, many forms of Hell exist that I will explain at the appropriate time. A crucial point to remember when studying Tavil is that the metaphor does not negate the original, nor does the original dismiss the metaphor. In other words, just because these 47,000 years symbolize paradise does not imply that actual paradise does not exist. What is paradise? Paradise is a place or state of bliss where all your desires are fulfilled, bringing you absolute contentment and peace. There is a spiritual paradise for the soul and a physical paradise for the body. 

 

 People born during this  period have a  strong affinity  for spirituality . During کشف , knowledge flows freely and abundantly through divine support ( تائید ) and inspiration  rather than traditional academic learning methods. High-level, extremely subtle, and sophisticated knowledge is shared openly, like the sun shining brightly for everyone. Scholars explain that gaining knowledge is similar to soaking up the sun, much like we do today. They describe it as a dream-like experience .  Just imagine how thrilling it must be to have a question and receive the perfect answer immediately from the Imam himself! Another remarkable aspect of this time is that individuals receive answers tailored precisely to their needs. Everyone has varying levels of tolerance for sunlight; some can bask in the sun's heat far more comfortably than others. Similarly, during this period, each person obtains answers to their inquiries based on their capabilities. This is the reason why spiritual growth blossoms at an exponential rate during this time. 

 

 The knowledge these people acquire is not veiled in physical and conceptual metaphors . In this دور ستر, we have to focus on physical and material knowledge, which then helps us form abstract ideas. For example, we first learn how the sun lights up the moon and then understand how the moon reflects that light. Once we know this, we can use it as a symbol to recognize that, just like the sun gives light, the ناطق possesses essential knowledge, and the أساس reflects that knowledge, much like the moon. We need to understand physical phenomena before connecting them to deeper spiritual ideas. This is why we have all these systematic and gradual physical practices to support our spiritual faith. 

 So, after a child first learns to pray five times a day, they are later taught the deeper esoteric meaning behind those actions and how they connect to their soul. That’s precisely what we’re doing now. We started with the physical story of Adam and are now using تاویل  to uncover its spiritual reality. In کشف , learning doesn’t happen in steps—it’s smooth, lightning-fast, and without debate. There are no arguments , disputes, or debates.  We argue when we are unsure or confused by different conflicting ideas in our minds, trying to figure out what’s right. But in کشف , there’s no wrong information, so there’s no conflict, confusion, or disagreement. 

 

 But here's the most important thing to remember: the knowledge disseminated during this time of کشف  is so subtle and lofty that it cannot be veiled into physical actions. This is why there are no physical obligations during this time. This is something people don't necessarily understand about کشف . It's not that the physical obligations are lifted as a favor or a blessing to create ease. They don't exist because the level of knowledge is so high and lofty that physical actions cannot veil it.

 

 This is also why no prophets and messengers are  required during this period. It's not that they don't exist; they're just not needed. Why? Because the sun is shining brightly in the sky. If you remember, we learned in the Book of Faith that stars represent the prophets and Dua'at. Just as the sun shines brightly and overshadows the moon and stars, the presence and knowledge of the Imam are so intense and readily available that these lower stations become unnecessary for guidance. That doesn't mean they don't exist. 

 
Everyone born in this time is a true believer ( مومنین ). They don’t have moments of doubt like we do. Disobedience, arrogance, and pride do not exist during this time. People during this time remain in perpetual awe, worship, and submission out of their own free will. In other words, everyone born during this time is a perfect believer. Now, what other creature does this description remind you of? Interestingly, this is the very description of angels in the Quran!

 

 

 The Bee  [16:49-50] 

 [ سورۃ ۱۶] النحل 

 ‏وَلِلَّهِ يَسْجُدُ مَا فِى ٱلسَّمَوَتِ وَمَا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مِن دَآبَّةٍۢ وَٱلْمَلَٓئِكَةُ وَهُمْ لَا يَسْتَكْبِرُونَ ‎ ۴۹ ‏يَخَافُونَ رَبَّهُم مِّن فَوْقِهِمْ وَيَفْعَلُونَ مَا يُؤْمَرُونَ ‎ ۵۰ 

 ( 49 ) And to Allah prostate all that is in the heavens and all that is in the earth, of the live moving creatures and the angels, and they are not proud. ( 50 ) They fear their Lord above them, and they do what they are commanded. 

 

 The Prophets  [21:27] 

 [ سورۃ ۲۱] الانبیائ 

 ‏لَا يَسْبِقُونَهُۥ بِٱلْقَوْلِ وَهُم بِأَمْرِهِۦ يَعْمَلُونَ ‎ ۲۷ 

 They speak not until He has spoken, and they act on His Command.

 Notice how the Quranic portrayal of angels perfectly aligns with the description of the people of Kashf. Angels are ethereal, spiritual, and radiant beings, free from all physical limitations and imperfections. They're beautifully depicted as winged creatures, symbolizing their elevated status in the spiritual realms of the Imam. Their ability to fly represents not only the heights of their knowledge, character, and spirituality but also the remarkable speed of their spiritual advancement.

 Jinn

 After these amazing 47,000 years of کشف , a new breed of people begins to emerge over the next 3,000 years, and they start developing a bit of an interest in materialism. While this inclination is very subtle compared to what we see today, the ideas begin to take root. This change gradually clouds mental clarity as people start to yearn for power, wealth, appearance, and social status. When the sun shines brightly overhead at its highest point (noon), objects don’t cast any shadows. But then, as it begins to set, objects start casting shadows, gradually growing longer as the sun continues its descent. Shadows are like footprints left behind when light encounters something in its way, just as evil appears where goodness is hindered or blocked. Darkness isn't an independent force; it only arises when light is blocked. A shadow is reality's illusion—a skewed outline that can sometimes be mistaken for the truth. Just as evil might disguise itself as power or deception, wearing the facade of wisdom, shadows can lead us on a chase towards emptiness, causing us to confuse absence with actual substance. In the same way, spiritual concepts begin to cast a shadow of physicality. 

 

 Despite this, they maintain a profound spiritual connection and recognize the importance of following the Imam for their spiritual growth. However, the aspiration to learn directly from the Imam devolves into a desire to become teachers themselves. Their total submission gradually transitions towards a subtle longing for self-reliance. Little by little, they begin to feel attracted to the comforts and pleasures of the material world. 

 

 It is essential to recognize that during the فترۃ  period, both types of personalities appear. Similar to the twilight phase, where light and darkness coexist, one group continues to uphold the angelic traits of کشف.  In contrast, the other group indicates a mental shift towards materialism and physical pleasure. At this time, no physical actions or commitments are necessary due to the elevated nature of the knowledge. 

 

 The Quran describes these individuals as جن , beings created from smokeless fire. Unlike نور  (light), which is calm, relaxing, and nurturing, fire نار  tends to be flashy and captivating with the dance of its flames. While fire may be catchy to the eye, it doesn’t possess the purity of light. The term “smokeless” suggests that these individuals had a very subtle material nature. Proper combustion of dense material is needed to produce fire.

 

 Man

 The conclusion of 3000 years of spiritual twilight ( فترۃ ) brings about the start of 7000 years of Concealment ( ستر ). This timeframe is known as "The Period of Concealment" because the Imam goes into complete hiding. The sun finally sets, and darkness prevails. The people who emerge during this time are focused heavily on materialism and possessions. The conceptual inclinations for materialism established over the past 3,000 years strengthened during this period.  This is the time when evil is allowed to prevail and thrive. Hence, the need for prophets arises, similar to the moon and stars appearing at night to guide travelers in the dark. Evil forces grow strong and establish their call toward atheism, agnosticism, materialism, consumerism, nudity, and sexual perversion in the name of freedom. All traits considered detestable in previous timeframes are celebrated and idolized during this period. Wealth, fame, and power, rather than knowledge, become the sought-after possessions. 

 For instance, studying history reveals that singers, dancers, entertainers, and jesters were once viewed as lesser professionals. Kings employed them to entertain the royals and the elite. In contrast, today, these individuals are considered celebrities and are revered. The values and beliefs surrounding them have been reversed. What was once considered flawed and evil is now regarded as good, while what was once regarded as good is now seen as bad. Virtues like piety, modesty, and righteousness have been overshadowed by lewdness, perversion, and immorality. 

 The shift in mindset transitions from submission to arrogance, moving from worshiping Allah to self-adulation. This fosters argumentative knowledge, resulting in confusion and mistrust. Various religions and sects emerge during this time, driven by personal desires, marking a period when evil seems to prevail. 

 Figures like فرعون، ھامان، ابو جھل، ابولھب appear, opposing the Imams and prophets. Similar to harmful reptiles, rodents, and insects that hide in the earth during the day, they emerge from their concealed places as night falls. This reflects the current state of the world. It's not that Allah has lost His control; it’s simply the designated time for evil to rise. During this era, truth weakens while falsehood thrives. Believers born in this time struggle to fortify their faith and are more susceptible to mistakes. This is why humans are termed انسان , derived from نسی , meaning "to forget." The numerous distractions and temptations of this world easily lead us to overlook the spiritual dimension of our existence. The soul is drawn to physical desires and pleasures, losing sight of spirituality. 

 This highlights the importance of having unwavering reminders from the Quran and our religious duties. This underlines why the spiritual laws of شریعۃ  have been structured and enforced over these 7000 years. 

 Summary

 

  

 

  

 

 

 

 

 SAT YUG, TRETA YUG, KAL YUG

---

## Session 7: The Story Of Adam (ZAHIR)

*Source: KSessions Group 18, Session ID 1281, 2700 words*

Story Of Adam (Zahir)

 Before mankind was created, Allah Almighty announced His divine plan to the angels, declaring His intention to place a successor, a Caliph, on Earth. 

 

 The Cow  [2:30] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ ۖ قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ ‎ ۳۰ 

 And when your Lord said to the angels: "Indeed, I am going to place my Caliph on earth." They said: "Will You place therein those who will make mischief therein and shed blood, - while we glorify You with praises and gratitude, and sanctify You." He said: "I know that which you do not know."

 Allah informs the angels of His intention to create a representative on Earth. This conversation occurs in the heavens, the realm of Allah (SWT) and His angels. This announcement prompts the angels to question Allah’s decision, expressing concerns that such a creation might lead to chaos on Earth. They point out that if it is praise and glorification He seeks, they are already fulfilling that role.

However, this doesn't align with how angels are portrayed in the Quran, right? In the Quran, they are portrayed as luminous beings in complete submission to their Creator! So why are they arguing with Allah here?
Nevertheless, they dispute with Allah, suggesting that creating a representative could be a mistake. Allah responds, stating, "I know what you do not know."

 Creation Of Adam

 Allah then decides to create Adam from clay. Clay is defined as a mixture of earth (silicon) and water. Prophetic traditions (Hadith) detail how this material was gathered, with angels reportedly sent to collect dust from various locations on Earth, resulting in a mixture of colors and types (red, white, black, and combinations, both soft and hard). This variety in the clay's origin is believed to account for the differences seen among Adam's descendants regarding appearance and temperament. From this clay, Allah directly shapes Adam with His two hands, as indicated in the verse addressed to Iblis:

 

 Sad  [38:75] 

 [ سورۃ ۳۸] ص 

 ‏قَالَ يَٓإِبْلِيسُ مَا مَنَعَكَ أَن تَسْجُدَ لِمَا خَلَقْتُ بِيَدَىَّ ۖ أَسْتَكْبَرْتَ أَمْ كُنتَ مِنَ ٱلْعَالِينَ ‎ ۷۵ 

  said: "O Iblîs  !! What prevents you from prostrating yourself to one whom I have created with Both My Hands . Are you too proud or are you one of the high exalted?"

 This is interesting! We understand that Allah (SWT) is entirely free from all forms of anthropomorphism, which means attributing human qualities or behaviors to Him. He is unique and unlike any of His creations— ليس كمثله شيء —signifying that nothing can be compared to Him. So, what does it mean that Allah created Adam with both hands? Does Allah have a left and a right hand like we do? In fact, it is mentioned in a prophetic narration that Rasul Allah (PBUH) said:

 إِنَّ الْمُقْسِطِينَ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ عَلَى مَنَابِرَ مِنْ نُورٍ عَنْ يَمِينِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ وَكِلْتَا يَدَيْهِ يَمِينٌ الَّذِينَ يَعْدِلُونَ فِي حُكْمِهِمْ وَأَهْلِيهِمْ وَمَا وَلُوا 
Indeed, the just will be upon pulpits of light near Allah, on the right-hand side of the Most Merciful. And both of His Hands are right.

The Quran mentions that Allah has two hands, and our Prophet, peace be upon him, clarifies that both hands are right, implying that He has no left hand. With these two right hands, Allah creates and shapes clay into the human form of Adam.

Narrations indicate that Adam's form lay lifeless for a time before the soul was breathed into him. It is said that Iblis would circle around and knock on Adam's lifeless body, producing a sound similar to that of striking a hollow sculpture. This made Iblis aware of this new creation's potential weakness and vulnerability, which later fueled his arrogance and disobedience.

 Allah then brought Adam to life by breathing the soul ( روح ) into him:

 

 Al-Hijr  [15:29] 

 [ سورۃ ۱۵] الحجر 

 ‏فَإِذَا سَوَّيْتُهُۥ وَنَفَخْتُ فِيهِ مِن رُّوحِى فَقَعُوا۟ لَهُۥ سَجِدِينَ ‎ ۲۹ 

 "So, when I have fashioned him completely and breathed into him the soul which I created for him, then fall down prostrating yourselves unto him."

 To demonstrate Adam's unique capability and honor, Allah taught him the names of all things

 

 The Cow  [2:31] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَعَلَّمَ آدَمَ ٱلْأَسْمَآءَ كُلَّهَا ثُمَّ عَرَضَهُمْ عَلَى ٱلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ فَقَالَ أَنۢبِـُٔونِى بِأَسْمَآءِ هَٓؤُلَآءِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَدِقِينَ ‎ ۳۱ 

 And He taught Adam all the names , then He showed them to the angels and said, "Tell Me the names of these if you are truthful."

 Scholars suggest that this was a unique form of knowledge that wasn't even bestowed upon the angels, but then they were asked about it. Have you ever encountered a situation like this, where one student learns the material, but different students are asked about what the first student has learned? It’s quite strange because the Quran clearly states that Allah taught all the names to Adam, not to the angels. So when He asked the angels to share what Adam had learned, how could they possibly provide the answers? It doesn’t seem to make sense! Yet, these angels, who certainly had the ability to question Allah—after all, they did express their concerns about His plan to create Adam on earth—remained silent during this examination. Isn’t that strange?

 So the angels, in their humility and honesty, acknowledged their limitations.

 

 The Cow  [2:32] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏قَالُوا۟ سُبْحَنَكَ لَا عِلْمَ لَنَآ إِلَّا مَا عَلَّمْتَنَآ ۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلْعَلِيمُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ ‎ ۳۲ 

 They said: "Glory be to You, we have no knowledge except what you have taught us. Verily, it is You, the All-Knower, the All-Wise."

  Allah then commanded Adam to inform the angels of the names, which he did, demonstrating the station Allah had bestowed upon him. 

 

 The Cow  [2:33] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏قَالَ يَٓـَٔادَمُ أَنۢبِئْهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ ۖ فَلَمَّآ أَنۢبَأَهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ قَالَ أَلَمْ أَقُل لَّكُمْ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ غَيْبَ ٱلسَّمَوَتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَأَعْلَمُ مَا تُبْدُونَ وَمَا كُنتُمْ تَكْتُمُونَ ‎ ۳۳ 

 He said: "O Adam! Inform them of their names," and when he had informed them of their names, He said: "Did I not tell you that I know the Ghaib  in the heavens and the earth, and I know what you reveal and what you have been concealing?"

 Following this demonstration of Adam's appointed role, Allah commanded the angels to prostrate before Adam as a sign of respect and acknowledgment of his station.

 

 The Cow  [2:34] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَإِذْ قُلْنَا لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ ٱسْجُدُوا۟ لِآدَمَ فَسَجَدُوٓا۟ إِلَّآ إِبْلِيسَ أَبَىٰ وَٱسْتَكْبَرَ وَكَانَ مِنَ ٱلْكَفِرِينَ ‎ ۳۴ 

 And when We said to the angels: "Prostrate yourselves before Adam.". And they prostrated except Iblîs  , he refused and was proud and was one of the disbelievers.

 Iblis (Satan), who was a Jinn among the angels, refused out of arrogance and racial pride. He argued that his superiority was based on his creation from fire, while Adam's was based on clay.

 

 The Heights  [7:12] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَ مَا مَنَعَكَ أَلَّا تَسْجُدَ إِذْ أَمَرْتُكَ ۖ قَالَ أَنَا۠ خَيْرٌۭ مِّنْهُ خَلَقْتَنِى مِن نَّارٍۢ وَخَلَقْتَهُۥ مِن طِينٍۢ ‎ ۱۲ 

  said: "What prevented you that you did not prostrate, when I commanded you?" Iblîs  said: "I am better than him, You created me from fire, and him You created from clay."

 This part of the story is quite intriguing! As I mentioned earlier, according to the prophetic narrations, Iblis circled the lifeless body of Adam, which was made out of clay. He knocked on it and realized it was hollow inside, suggesting that it was a delicate creature since something hollow could easily break. Considering that Iblis is made of fire, which is seen as superior to clay, it’s interesting to reflect on this. Logically speaking, fire does indeed hold an advantage over the Earth. Fire brings brightness, while clay appears dull, showing that brightness is generally more appealing than dullness.
Additionally, the flames of fire rise toward the sky, contrasting with dust that falls to the ground, implying that elements that ascend are superior to those that descend. Moreover, fire is hot, and the earth is cold, indicating that heat holds strength compared to coldness. Therefore, Iblis had a point in saying he was superior to Adam and questioning why he should prostrate to him — shouldn’t Adam be the one showing him respect? Even though Iblis disobeyed Allah, he seemed to have a logical basis for his thoughts. This raises a thought-provoking question: why would Allah, who is known to be always just, ask a superior being to prostrate to one considered inferior? And why was Iblis punished for his refusal?

 Creation of Hawwa

 Prophetic Hadith indicates she was created from Adam's rib, likely his left rib, while he slept. This ensured he would not feel any pain and would awaken to discover her as a companion, alleviating his solitude. Adam was created directly by Allah from various earthly clays, shaped by His two Hands, and animated with the divine breath of a soul. Subsequently, Hawwa was made from Adam's rib to serve as his partner and companion. 

 Once again, it’s a bit puzzling. How can a woman come from a rib? And why a rib specifically? Instead of something else? So, Adam falls into a deep sleep, and his rib is gently removed to create his beloved wife, Hawwa. When he awakens, he sees this beautiful woman that Allah has wonderfully crafted for him. What was the purpose of Hawwa?

 

 The Heights  [7:189] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏ هُوَ ٱلَّذِى خَلَقَكُم مِّن نَّفْسٍۢ وَحِدَةٍۢ وَجَعَلَ مِنْهَا زَوْجَهَا لِيَسْكُنَ إِلَيْهَا ۖ فَلَمَّا تَغَشَّىٰهَا حَمَلَتْ حَمْلًا خَفِيفًۭا فَمَرَّتْ بِهِۦ ۖ فَلَمَّآ أَثْقَلَت دَّعَوَا ٱللَّهَ رَبَّهُمَا لَىِٕنْ آتَيْتَنَا صَلِحًۭا لَّنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ ٱلشَّكِرِينَ ‎ ۱۸۹ 

 It is He Who has created you from a single person, and He has created from him his wife, in order that he might enjoy the pleasure of living with her.

 So Hawwa was created so Adam could find companionship in her. Then Allah says:

 

 The Cow  [2:35] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَقُلْنَا يَٓـَٔادَمُ ٱسْكُنْ أَنتَ وَزَوْجُكَ ٱلْجَنَّةَ وَكُلَا مِنْهَا رَغَدًا حَيْثُ شِئْتُمَا وَلَا تَقْرَبَا هَذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةَ فَتَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلظَّلِمِينَ ‎ ۳۵ 

 And We said: "O Adam! Dwell you and your wife in the Paradise and eat both of you freely with pleasure and delight of things therein as wherever you will, but come not near this tree or you both will be of the Zalimun  ."

 In this beautiful paradise, Allah invites both Adam and Hawwa to enjoy all its wonders, with one exception: they should steer clear of a specific tree. They are reminded that approaching this tree would lead them to be counted among the unjust. This raises a curious question: why was this tree present in Paradise if Adam and Hawwa weren’t meant to access it? Some religious scholars suggest that the tree was harmful or evil. If so, why would it exist in a glorious place like paradise? Conversely, if it was good, what was the reason for forbidding Adam and Hawwa from it? Some believe it was the tree of wheat, while the Bible states it was an apple. It's interesting to note that what was once forbidden to Adam and his wife is utterly permissible for the children of Adam. Right? Both wheat and apples are Halal. So what happened there?

 They were forbidden to approach this specific tree. But Iblis, now their sworn enemy, sought revenge. He approached them with deceitful whispers, tempting them to approach the forbidden tree.

 

 The Heights  [7:20-21] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏فَوَسْوَسَ لَهُمَا ٱلشَّيْطَنُ لِيُبْدِىَ لَهُمَا مَا وُۥرِىَ عَنْهُمَا مِن سَوْءَتِهِمَا وَقَالَ مَا نَهَىٰكُمَا رَبُّكُمَا عَنْ هَذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةِ إِلَّآ أَن تَكُونَا مَلَكَيْنِ أَوْ تَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلْخَلِدِينَ ‎ ۲۰ ‏وَقَاسَمَهُمَآ إِنِّى لَكُمَا لَمِنَ ٱلنَّصِحِينَ ‎ ۲۱ 

 ( 20 ) Then Satan tempted them in order to expose what was hidden of their nakedness. He said, “Your Lord has forbidden this tree to you only to prevent you from becoming angels or immortals.” ( 21 ) And he swore to them, “I am truly your sincere advisor.” 

 Iblis shares with Adam that Allah doesn't want him to go near that tree because He wants to keep Adam from becoming an angel. You see, Adam was created from clay, while angels are made from luminous light. Iblis tells Adam that if he eats from this tree, he'll transform from clay into light and have the chance to live in paradise forever. He even swears to Adam that he truly wishes the best for him. Many scholars believe this was the key reason Adam was deceived; he couldn't imagine that someone would take an oath in Allah's name and then lie. So, Iblis cleverly tricks them into eating from the tree. 

 

 The Heights  [7:22] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏فَدَلَّىٰهُمَا بِغُرُورٍۢ ۚ فَلَمَّا ذَاقَا ٱلشَّجَرَةَ بَدَتْ لَهُمَا سَوْءَتُهُمَا وَطَفِقَا يَخْصِفَانِ عَلَيْهِمَا مِن وَرَقِ ٱلْجَنَّةِ ۖ وَنَادَىٰهُمَا رَبُّهُمَآ أَلَمْ أَنْهَكُمَا عَن تِلْكُمَا ٱلشَّجَرَةِ وَأَقُل لَّكُمَآ إِنَّ ٱلشَّيْطَنَ لَكُمَا عَدُوٌّۭ مُّبِينٌۭ ‎ ۲۲ 

 So he brought about their fall through deception. And when they tasted of the tree, their nakedness was exposed to them, prompting them to cover themselves with leaves from Paradise. Then their Lord called out to them, “Did I not forbid you from that tree and ?did I not? tell you that Satan is your sworn enemy?”

 This raises some important questions! What does eating from the tree have to do with them losing their garments? In our everyday experience, when someone makes a mistake, we usually don't strip them of their clothes, right? So why would Allah, who describes Himself as the Most Merciful, do this to Adam? It feels shameful. I certainly wouldn't treat even my worst enemy like that. Then, we witness a moment of helplessness and desperation in this verse as our parents, Adam and Hawwa, try to cover their nakedness with tree leaves. It truly is quite puzzling! But then, Allah expresses His disappointment and says to them, "Did I not forbid you from that tree, and did I not tell you that Iblis is your sworn enemy?" Filled with remorse, they turned to Allah in repentance, acknowledging their mistake.

 

 The Heights  [7:23] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَا رَبَّنَا ظَلَمْنَآ أَنفُسَنَا وَإِن لَّمْ تَغْفِرْ لَنَا وَتَرْحَمْنَا لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ ٱلْخَسِرِينَ ‎ ۲۳ 

 They said: "Our Lord! We have wronged ourselves. If You forgive us not, and bestow not upon us Your Mercy, we shall certainly be of the losers."

 Allah, in His infinite Mercy, taught Adam words of repentance and forgave them.

 

 The Cow  [2:37] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏فَتَلَقَّىٰٓ آدَمُ مِن رَّبِّهِۦ كَلِمَتٍۢ فَتَابَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ هُوَ ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ ‎ ۳۷ 

 Then Adam received from his Lord Words. And his Lord pardoned him. Verily, He is the One Who forgives, the Most Merciful.

 

 

 The Heights  [7:24-25] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَ ٱهْبِطُوا۟ بَعْضُكُمْ لِبَعْضٍ عَدُوٌّۭ ۖ وَلَكُمْ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مُسْتَقَرٌّۭ وَمَتَعٌ إِلَىٰ حِينٍۢ ‎ ۲۴ ‏قَالَ فِيهَا تَحْيَوْنَ وَفِيهَا تَمُوتُونَ وَمِنْهَا تُخْرَجُونَ ‎ ۲۵ 

 ( 24 ) [ Allah ] said, "Descend, being to one another enemies. And for you on the earth is a place of settlement and enjoyment for a time." ( 25 ) He said: "Therein you shall live, and therein you shall die, and from it you shall be brought out." 

 Though forgiven, the consequence of their action was their descent from Paradise to Earth, which was destined to be the dwelling place for them and their progeny, a place of life, death, and eventual resurrection. This also established the ongoing struggle between humanity and Shaytan. 

 Let's take a moment to reflect. How is this our fault? Why should we, the children of Adam, face punishment for our parents' sins? We didn't eat from the tree. We obeyed Allah. So, why are we exiled from paradise? It's possible that if we had been created in paradise and faced the same test, some—or even all—of us would have succeeded. Unfortunately, we never had that opportunity due to a mistake made by our parents, and that doesn’t seem fair at all. 

 Thus began the story of humanity on Earth, marked by the legacy of Adam's repentance, the ongoing enmity of Shaytan, and the continuous promise of Divine guidance for those who seek it.

---

## Session 8: The Story Of Adam (BATIN)

*Source: KSessions Group 18, Session ID 1283, 7666 words*

Story Of Adam (BATIN)

 With this context, let's revisit Adam's (AS) Story. The 47,000 years of کشف have come to an end, followed by the concluding 3,000 years of فتر . It was now time to begin the 7,000-year period of ستر . The name of the Imam at that time was Imam Hunaid (AS), referred to as "Allah" in the story. He knew it was time for the Imams to go into hiding, and guidance would be disseminated through a spiritual hierarchy of prophets and messengers. He was looking for someone for the role of Adam. The term Adam is not a person's name. It represents a title for the individual who ushers in a new era - the beginner of the period of Sitr. 

 Imam Hunaid (AS) had a nephew named Abdullah, who was still studying the fundamentals of religion. Recognizing signs of prophethood in this young man, the Imam decided to designate him the first prophet or Adam of دور ستر. Imam. So he turns to the learned scholars of his time and says

 ‏وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ

 When Allah, meaning Imam Hunaid, said to the esteemed scholars of his time—illustrated as "angels"—the time of darkness (ستر) had arrived. It's time for Imams to go into hiding and conceal themselves. However, the process of guidance must continue. Therefore, I have decided to appoint my nephew Abdullah as my representative ( خلیفۃ ). In response, they replied:

 قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ 

  They said will you place there someone who will sow corruption and spill blood?

 What does corruption ( فساد ) mean? Corruption is a deviation from the original intent or meaning, resulting in something inappropriate or diminished. Remember, these individuals were beings of فترۃ , spiritually elevated. They had an innate spiritual knowledge that didn't require physical reminders. They knew that subtle knowledge ( علم لدنی ) is not acquired through traditional education; instead, it is attained through divine support and inspiration ( تائیدات ). Due to its elevated nature, it cannot conform to the physical realm. 

 In contrast, dense knowledge ( ظاہر ) necessitates actions to sustain itself. This requirement introduces the burden of responsibility and accountability—something they felt Abdullah (Adam) could not handle. Thus, they warned Imam Hunaid that despite his best efforts, he would struggle to fulfill his duties. He is too young for the job and lacks any spiritual knowledge. He will inevitably encounter challenges and make mistakes, and his shortcomings will lead to spiritual corruption. Ultimately, he will distort and corrupt spirituality. 

 In esoteric interpretation, " spilling  blood " symbolizes the failure to conquer  the lower self ( نفس الامارۃ  - the soul that commands evil) , which encompasses basic instincts, passions, ignorance, and, crucially, doubt. Doubt poses the greatest obstacle to spiritual awakening and the attainment of certainty ( یقین ) in faith and knowledge. 

 These individuals told the Imam that because Abdullah is not trained in spirituality, he will struggle to align physical actions with spirituality and ultimately foster doubt within himself and others. Blood symbolizes doubt or spiritual weakness in Taveel. 

 وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ

 And we are enough to praise and glorify you! We don't need physical actions as a means of remembrance or a tool to grasp spiritual truths. We are the ones who can genuinely appreciate the profound wisdom and knowledge that you have imparted and bestowed upon us. Because of our clarity, we are less susceptible to making mistakes in our praise, gratitude, and glorification of you. So, if you want to select someone to go and spread the word, choose from Among Us. We are much better suited for the job, not Abdullah. 

 They weren't directly challenging the Imam; instead, they were puzzled by his choice of Abdullah, whom they deemed inadequate due to his limited spiritual training. They wondered why he wouldn't select someone more knowledgeable and competent from among them. Their love for the Imam was immense, making it hard for them to tolerate any lack of praise, gratitude, or reverence directed towards him.  

 To that, Imam Hunaid responded:  قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ 

 Imam Hunaid (AS) said, "I know that which you do not know." This is the suspense in the scene, which reveals itself later in the story. Now, let's reread this verse through the lens of Taveel.

 

 

 The Cow  [2:30] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ ۖ قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ ‎ ۳۰ 

 When Imam

 When Imam Hunaid (AS) announced to the esteemed scholars of his time (angels), "I will appoint my nephew Abdullah as my representative for جزائر  during the period of Sitr," they replied, "Abdullah is too young and inexperienced; he lacks the spiritual knowledge needed for this role. He won’t be able to handle the responsibility and will likely fall short in providing guidance, which will create doubt and corrupt spirituality. We, however, are well-versed in spiritual sciences and can properly venerate your name. So why not choose one of us?" Imam Hunaid replied, "I know what you do not know." 

 Creation of Adam

 The Quran mentions that he started creating Adam with his two hands from clay. In esoteric teachings, soil, or تراب , symbolizes the essence of a believer, as it is pure and capable of transformation. It has the potential to nurture and cultivate any seeds sown in it into a fruit-bearing tree. Soil represents the pure essence of a believer . In Surah an-Naba, where Allah (SWT) states in the final verse:

 إِنَّا أَنذَرْنَاكُمْ عَذَابًا قَرِيبًا ۖ يَوْمَ يَنظُرُ الْمَرْءُ مَا قَدَّمَتْ يَدَاهُ وَيَقُولُ الْكَافِرُ يَا لَيْتَنِي كُنتُ تُرَابًا

 Indeed, We have warned you of a near punishment on the Day when a man will observe what his hands have put forth, and the disbeliever will say, "Oh, I wish that I were dust!

 Many interpretations of this verse exist, particularly concerning the terms "dust" or "soil." However, its true meaning is that on Judgment Day, the disbeliever will lament, "Woe unto me, I wish I had been a believer today."

 So, تراب  represents the pure essence of a believer, and water is knowledge of تاویل . When you mix soil ( تراب ) and water ( ماء ), what do you get? You get Clay ( طین ).  As discussed in the Book of Faith, every imam has a council of 12 esteemed figures known as his حدود . From these 12, Imam Hunaid (AS) chose two prominent scholars to educate and mentor his nephew in the sciences of Shareeah and Taveel. They were the two "hands" of "Allah". The left hand was the teacher assigned to teach Adam (AS) Shareeah, while the right was the teacher assigned to teach him Taveel. We know the right hand is superior to the left, just as Taveel's knowledge is superior to Shareeah's. Why are they called hands? Because it is our hands that do all the work. Imam Hunaid appointed these two teachers to train Adam and prepare him for his role. Just as soil mixed with water becomes clay and can be molded into any shape, these two teachers of Adam began molding and sculpting Adam spiritually with the knowledge of Zahir and Batin.

 While all this is happening, Imam Hunaid chose the 12 individuals for Adam (AS) and gave them the following instructions:

 

 Al-Hijr  [15:29] 

 [ سورۃ ۱۵] الحجر 

 ‏فَإِذَا سَوَّيْتُهُۥ وَنَفَخْتُ فِيهِ مِن رُّوحِى فَقَعُوا۟ لَهُۥ سَجِدِينَ ‎ ۲۹ 

 "So, when I have fashioned him completely and breathed into him the soul which I created for him, then fall down prostrating yourselves unto him."

 Once Adam (AS) has received comprehensive training in Shareah and Taveel, and I have infused him with my essence, you are all required to prostrate before him, confirming your submission to his leadership. Infusing him with my essence meant imparting the secrets, roles, and responsibilities that pertain directly to the station of the Speaker ناطق . The Speakers are gifted the power of speech and expression by Allah(SWT), which enables them to formulate a Shariah or religious constitution for the people of their time. So that's what Imam Hunaid was saying. That once I train Abdullah for the post of Natiq, وَنَفَخْتُ فِيهِ مِن رُّوحِى فَقَعُوا۟ لَهُۥ سَجِدِينَ , all of you should submit to him. Who is Imam Hunaid speaking to? The twelve members that Adam AS was going to lead.

 Among these twelve people was a highly knowledgeable man and an esteemed scholar, proficient not just in the sciences of Zahir and Batin but also Haqaiq. His name was Haris. He had been appointed as the teacher for the other eleven by Imam Hunaid. His title was "معلم الملکوت ," the teacher of angels. As described in the Quran, he belonged to the Jinn because he nurtured ambitions for power and status. Haris aspired to begin this new era of ستر  and wanted to have the honor of becoming the Adam of this time. He gets threatened when he hears Imam Hunaid has chosen Abdullah for the post.

 Testing the Angels

 The narration explains that Iblis would walk around Adam's lifeless body and tap it, only to hear a sound like that of an empty vessel. This means that during Adam's training, he would pose questions that demanded Taveel's responses. However, a student learning this knowledge isn’t permitted to discuss it until given the go-ahead by his superior. Thus, whenever Haris inquired about something, Abdullah would respond from the apparent knowledge ( ظاھر ). This led Haris to conclude that Adam's understanding was shallow and lacking depth, suggesting he was unaware of the Taveel regarding the external facets of religion - hence the hollow sound. The narration also describes him circling Adam's lifeless form, meaning he approached him from various angles on different subjects. When Adam did not respond with esoteric insights, Haris assumed his knowledge was inadequate and lacking depth. This intensified his hostility, jealousy, and arrogance, reinforcing his belief that he was superior to Adam.

 

 The Cow  [2:31] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَعَلَّمَ آدَمَ ٱلْأَسْمَآءَ كُلَّهَا ثُمَّ عَرَضَهُمْ عَلَى ٱلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ فَقَالَ أَنۢبِـُٔونِى بِأَسْمَآءِ هَٓؤُلَآءِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَدِقِينَ ‎ ۳۱ 

 And He taught Adam all the names , then He showed them to the angels and said, "Tell Me the names of these if you are truthful."

 Imam Hunaid (AS) taught Adam all the names. What exactly is a name? A name serves to identify an individual or an object. Teaching Adam the names represents the power to reveal and clarify hidden spiritual truths within physical actions. The “names” (أسماء) are symbols for deep spiritual realities, divine attributes, knowledge, and the profound truths (haqa’iq) hidden behind the physical universe. These truths were the unseen realities ( ghayb ), the deeper essence behind apparent forms, representing inner spiritual knowledge (ʿilm al-batin). The act of naming symbolizes unveiling (kashf) , revealing esoteric knowledge through the Imam’s inspired wisdom. 

 After Adam completed his training, Imam Hunaid presented him to the angels. Angels, in this context, symbolize the twelve members of Adam's cabinet who were chosen to assist him. These individuals were pure yet limited in their spiritual perception. They were obedient, luminous beings representing followers who require guidance from a higher intellect (the Imam) to achieve a deeper understanding. The angels’ inability to name these truths signifies the spiritual limitations inherent in the individual soul until it is awakened and guided by a higher spiritual authority.

 He then asked, أَنۢبِـُٔونِى بِأَسْمَآءِ هَٓؤُلَآءِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَدِقِينَ  - 'Tell Me the names of these if you are truthful.' Truthful about what, exactly? Remember, these beings had previously claimed they were prepared and capable of taking on the role of prophethood during the time of Sitr. Imam Hunaid (AS) challenged them to explain how they would establish the hierarchy of prophets and messengers. How would they guide humanity out of ignorance? How would they elevate them from materialism and physicality to spirituality? They did not know this, as they had never encountered such a scenario before. They responded:

 

 The Cow  [2:32] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏قَالُوا۟ سُبْحَنَكَ لَا عِلْمَ لَنَآ إِلَّا مَا عَلَّمْتَنَآ ۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلْعَلِيمُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ ‎ ۳۲ 

 They said: "Glory be to You, we have no knowledge except what you have taught us. Verily, it is You, the All-Knower, the All-Wise."

 They said we now realize that we do not know everything. We only know what you have taught us and we are arrogant in thinking we know it all.  إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلْعَلِيمُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ  indeed we admit that you alone are the all-knowing and the all-wise. 

 

 The Cow  [2:33] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ
