 ‏قَالَ يَٓـَٔادَمُ أَنۢبِئْهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ ۖ فَلَمَّآ أَنۢبَأَهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ قَالَ أَلَمْ أَقُل لَّكُمْ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ غَيْبَ ٱلسَّمَوَتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَأَعْلَمُ مَا تُبْدُونَ وَمَا كُنتُمْ تَكْتُمُونَ ‎ ۳۳ 

 He said: "O Adam! Inform them of their names," and when he had informed them of their names, He said: "Did I not tell you that I know the Ghaib  in the heavens and the earth, and I know what you reveal and what you have been concealing?"

 Imam Hunaid commanded Abdullah to explain the workings of the upcoming period of darkness. Adam imparted his knowledge, teaching insights these people previously lacked, demonstrating his superior status as their teacher. Imam Hunaid stated, "Did I not tell you that I know all that is hidden in the heavens and the earth? 

 This underscores the role of divine guidance and the concept of wilayah (spiritual guardianship) . It reinforces the idea that ultimate reality and hidden truths reside with the Divine, communicated through chosen intermediaries (Imams). True knowledge ( ʿilm al-ghayb ) of heavens and earth—the spiritual and material domains—is disclosed by Imams through His appointed guides.

 Example/Metaphor: Consider a dark room filled with valuable treasures. Without illumination (guidance), one may wander endlessly, ignorant of the room's riches. The Imam, holding divine knowledge, is the torchbearer who illuminates the hidden treasures, transforming darkness into understanding.

  The verse also represents the tajalli (manifestation) of Divine attributes. Adam’s naming is symbolic of the Imam manifesting these divine attributes and qualities (like wisdom, mercy, and knowledge) to humanity. This teaching signifies transforming potential spiritual knowledge (implicit in angels) into realized spiritual knowledge (explicit in Adam) .

 The phrase "I know what you reveal and conceal" emphasizes the duality of zahir (outer) and batin (inner) realities. This means the Imam not only guides believers outwardly but also has insight into their inner states and hidden spiritual conditions.

 Example/Metaphor: Think of a gardener who not only sees the surface appearance of plants but also understands the unseen roots below. Similarly, the Imam’s guidance touches both the outer (behavioral) and inner (spiritual) dimensions of human beings.

 As for the angels, what was the ظاہر  they were manifesting? Their sacrifice to take on the hardships of leading the people of Sitr. What were they hiding? The fact that they thought they were better than Abdullah in their knowledge and spirituality.

 

 

 The Cow  [2:34] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَإِذْ قُلْنَا لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ ٱسْجُدُوا۟ لِآدَمَ فَسَجَدُوٓا۟ إِلَّآ إِبْلِيسَ أَبَىٰ وَٱسْتَكْبَرَ وَكَانَ مِنَ ٱلْكَفِرِينَ ‎ ۳۴ 

 And when We said to the angels: "Prostrate yourselves before Adam.". And they prostrated except Iblîs  , he refused and was proud and was one of the disbelievers.

 He then commanded these 12 people, represented by angels, to prostrate to Adam - meaning he told them to submit to Adam's leadership. They immediately accepted their weakness and Adam's superiority over them based on the knowledge that he had acquired, and they immediately fell into prostration. This doesn't mean they fell on their hands and feet in front of him. Prostration is the ultimate sign of submission because we place our head containing our intellect, thoughts, pride, arrogance, plans, and schemes on the floor. In other words, everyone acknowledged Adam as their leader except for Haris. Driven by pride and arrogance, he turned away, becoming one of the کافرین . The term کفر  signifies concealment; a farmer is referred to as a کافر  because he buries and hides the seed underground. Haris was fully aware of Adam's superiority but deliberately obscured this truth. 

 

 The Heights  [7:12] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَ مَا مَنَعَكَ أَلَّا تَسْجُدَ إِذْ أَمَرْتُكَ ۖ قَالَ أَنَا۠ خَيْرٌۭ مِّنْهُ خَلَقْتَنِى مِن نَّارٍۢ وَخَلَقْتَهُۥ مِن طِينٍۢ ‎ ۱۲ 

  said: "What prevented you that you did not prostrate, when I commanded you?" Iblîs  said: "I am better than him, You created me from fire, and him You created from clay."

 Imam Hunaid (AS) asks Haris why he chose not  to accept Adam as his leader despite Adam proving his superiority and Imam Hunaid commanding him to do so. Haris replied, "I am better than him! I am made from fire, and he is made from clay. You have taught me the subtle knowledge of deep realities (حقائق) that cannot coexist with physical forms. In contrast, Adam only knows Shariah and Taveel, which is dense, logical, natural, and argumentative knowledge. How can I bow to someone inferior to me? Therefore, I am better than him."

 

 Al-Hijr  [15:34-35] 

 [ سورۃ ۱۵] الحجر 

 ‏قَالَ فَٱخْرُجْ مِنْهَا فَإِنَّكَ رَجِيمٌۭ ‎ ۳۴ ‏وَإِنَّ عَلَيْكَ ٱللَّعْنَةَ إِلَىٰ يَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ ‎ ۳۵ 

 ( 34 ) said: "Then, get out from here, for verily, you are Rajîm  ."  ( 35 ) "And verily, the curse shall be upon you till the Day of Recompense." 

 Imam Hunaid expels Haris from his presence, subjecting him to humiliation. The term رجم  means to stone someone. If you've seen depictions of stoning in older films, they typically show a person running while being pursued and bombarded with stones. This imagery symbolizes the act of expelling someone from a particular area. This instance signifies that the eleven members who accepted Adam as their leader rejected Haris and severed their ties with him. Imam Hunaid then declared to Haris, وَإِنَّ عَلَيْكَ ٱللَّعْنَةَ إِلَىٰ يَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ - you are cursed until the Day of Judgment. The term لعنۃ  translates to "to banish, expel, or remove from grace." Until this moment, these individuals were receiving their knowledge and blessings through divine support ( تائیدات ), which is referred to in the Quran as " رحمۃ " (blessing). Imam Hunaid terminated this divine support from Haris, informing him that neither he nor his followers would be granted support until the end of the 7,000 years when Qaim arrived.

 Recall when Imam Hunaid told the angels, "I know what you conceal and reveal from what's within your hearts"? Scholars explain it meant two things:

 What these angels hid was their belief that Allah would not create anyone superior to them and then venerate him with prostration. 

 Additionally, Imam Hunaid pointed out that someone among you is repressing his arrogance, pride, and jealousy. While he pretends to be submissive, he truly wishes to dominate. By selecting Adam as the first prophet of this era, Imam Hunaid revealed Haris’s hidden malicious intentions. This is why he stated, "وَأَعْلَمُ مَا تُبْدُونَ وَمَا كُنتُمْ تَكْتُمُونَ"—I know what you reveal and what you conceal. Harris portrays himself as a devoted servant to his master, yet he secretly longs for unearned power that could ultimately lead to the downfall of humanity. He is called "Iblis" because it comes from the root word ابلس, which means to despair or to be cut off. He was cut off from the mercy (تائیدات) of Imam Hunaid and therefore called ابلیس. 

 The difference here is that even though all 12 harbored a superiority complex within their hearts, 11 immediately accepted their fault and submitted to the will of the imam. In contrast, one refused out of his arrogance and pride. 

 Establishment of Kaaba

 Imam Al-Sadiq (peace be upon him) shared a beautiful story about a man who approached his father to learn about the origins of the sacred House, the Kaaba. He shared, "When Allah revealed Adam's superior knowledge to the angels, they realized the weight of their mistake. Filled with remorse, they sought refuge at the Divine Throne, circling it for seven days, sincerely asking for their Lord’s forgiveness, which He graciously granted. 

 Then, He instructed them: ‘Descend to the Earth and construct a House, a sanctuary where anyone among Adam’s descendants who have strayed may find shelter. Let them circumambulate it, just as you sought comfort around My Throne, and I shall be pleased with them, just as I was pleased with you.’

 Thus, they built that original House. Later, during the Great Flood of نوح, Allah, in His splendor, elevated it to the fourth heaven, where it continues to reside. There, the angels perform their pilgrimage, a holy rite that will endure until the Day of Resurrection. 

 On Earth, Ibrahim, the beloved friend of Allah (Al-Khalil), rebuilt this earthly House on its original, ancient foundations."

 Challenge of Iblis

 So now that Haris becomes Iblis, he challenges Imam Hunaid and says, "You dishonored me, and so my revenge to you will be that I will make sure Adam and his progeny also get dishonored and removed from paradise." 

 

 The Children of Israel  [17:61-64] 

 [ سورۃ ۱۷] الاسرا 

 ‏وَإِذْ قُلْنَا لِلْمَلَٓئِكَةِ ٱسْجُدُوا۟ لِآدَمَ فَسَجَدُوٓا۟ إِلَّآ إِبْلِيسَ قَالَ ءَأَسْجُدُ لِمَنْ خَلَقْتَ طِينًۭا ‎ ۶۱ ‏قَالَ أَرَءَيْتَكَ هَذَا ٱلَّذِى كَرَّمْتَ عَلَىَّ لَىِٕنْ أَخَّرْتَنِ إِلَىٰ يَوْمِ ٱلْقِيَمَةِ لَأَحْتَنِكَنَّ ذُرِّيَّتَهُۥٓ إِلَّا قَلِيلًۭا ‎ ۶۲ ‏قَالَ ٱذْهَبْ فَمَن تَبِعَكَ مِنْهُمْ فَإِنَّ جَهَنَّمَ جَزَآؤُكُمْ جَزَآءًۭ مَّوْفُورًۭا ‎ ۶۳ ‏وَٱسْتَفْزِزْ مَنِ ٱسْتَطَعْتَ مِنْهُم بِصَوْتِكَ وَأَجْلِبْ عَلَيْهِم بِخَيْلِكَ وَرَجِلِكَ وَشَارِكْهُمْ فِى ٱلْأَمْوَلِ وَٱلْأَوْلَدِ وَعِدْهُمْ ۚ وَمَا يَعِدُهُمُ ٱلشَّيْطَنُ إِلَّا غُرُورًا ‎ ۶۴ 

 And ˹remember˺ when We said to the angels, “Prostrate before Adam,” so they all did—but not Iblîs, who protested, “Should I prostrate to the one You have created from mud?”
Adding, “Do you see this one you honored above me? If you delay my end until the Day of Judgment, I will certainly take hold of his descendants, except for a few.”
Allah responded, “Be gone! Whoever of them follows you, Hell will surely be the reward for all of you—an ample reward.
And incite whoever you can of them with your voice, mobilize against them all your cavalry and infantry, manipulate them in their wealth and children, and make them promises.” But Satan promises them nothing but delusion.

 So Haris challenged Imam Hunaid and said, "Give me until the day of judgment, or in other words, give me and my descendants free reign for this entire 7000 years to misguide and mislead whoever and however we want until the arrival of Qaim (AS)." Imam Hunaid accepted the challenge and said, "Fine! Go ahead! But remember that whoever follows you will be considered your children and not the children of Adam."

 This is quite fascinating, isn't it? What prompted this allowance? Wouldn't it have benefitted us if Iblis had been stopped right then and there? So, what is the rationale behind granting Iblis this reprieve? The first reason is that the period of Sitr was beginning, characterized by the proliferation of evil, with Iblis serving as the primary catalyst. Additionally, the intent was to ensure a consistent testing of humanity. Imam Hunaid said to Haris that guidance will continually be given to the children of Adam, yet your whispers and temptations will test them to determine who remains steadfast in their faith. 

 In esoteric terms, "children of Adam" signifies children of faith. Adam is viewed as the spiritual father, while Hawwa is considered the spiritual mother. Their teachings on Zahir and Batin nurture believers' souls, preparing them for paradise in the afterlife. Just as Allah's hands shaped Adam, so too will the children of Adam undergo a similar process. The Imams will establish religious hierarchies to guide the souls of believers, sculpting their souls through the teachings of Shariah and Taveel, defining the limits of halal and haram. Just as Satan tempted our parents, he will similarly test the resolve of believers. Their task is to resist temptation and adhere to Allah's guidance. Yet, understanding the inherent weakness of humans, Allah recognizes that mistakes are inevitable. During these critical moments, the children of Adam will be presented with two options, as shown in the narrative: they can follow in their parent's footsteps—embracing repentance, recognizing their mistakes, and turning back to Allah—or they may opt for pride, greed, and jealousy, ultimately choosing the path of Satan. 

 So Imam Hunaid grants Satan this reprieve he says   

 ‏وَٱسْتَفْزِزْ مَنِ ٱسْتَطَعْتَ مِنْهُم بِصَوْتِكَ وَأَجْلِبْ عَلَيْهِم بِخَيْلِكَ وَرَجِلِكَ وَشَارِكْهُمْ فِى ٱلْأَمْوَلِ وَٱلْأَوْلَدِ وَعِدْهُمْ ۚ وَمَا يَعِدُهُمُ ٱلشَّيْطَنُ إِلَّا غُرُورًا

 "And incite [to evil] whoever you can among them with your voice, and assault them with your horses and foot soldiers, and become a partner in their wealth and their children."

 "Incite... with your voice" :

 Esoterically, Satan's "voice" is the misleading inner promptings of the lower self (نفس) , which can sow doubt, encourage our most base desires, and promote our attachment to the material world (dunya). 

 It also represents those false doctrines, materialistic philosophies, or any expressions or teachings  that guide people away from the true spiritual path (Haqq).from the true spiritual path (Haqq).

 

 "Assault them with your horses ( خیل ) and foot soldiers ( رجل )" :

 "Horses" (khaya), often seen as symbols of power and speed, represent stronger, more overt temptations or opposing forces that challenge believers.  These can include intense worldly ambitions, overwhelming passions, or organized groups that actively hinder the spread of genuine spiritual awareness and community among believers. 

 

 On the other hand, "foot soldiers" (rajil) symbolize the subtler, insidious influences of evil. This can mean internal weaknesses, negative habits, becoming gradually desensitized to sin, or even individuals who, while less noticeable, sow seeds of doubt, corruption, and deviation from authentic faith.

 Together, they embody the complex arguments, deceits, and agents of Iblis (whether from humans or lower impulses) that wage a constant battle against the human soul and its intellect ('aql) on its journey toward spiritual growth and fulfillment.

 

 "Become a partner in their wealth ( اموال ) and their children ( اولاد )" :

 Wealth (اموال): Literally, this refers to the wealth acquired through illegal means or the inappropriate use of any God-given resources and blessings—whether they are our material possessions, intellectual talents, or spiritual gifts—when they serve our lower selves, worldly desires, or hinder spiritual growth.  Esoterically, wealth represents knowledge, which means I will distort their correct understanding with false teachings by instilling doubt or altering the interpretations of the Quran and Hadith to align with their passions, desires, and innovations. In essence, this signifies being ensnared by the love of Dunya, elevating it to an object of worship.

 

 Children (اولاد): Beyond just the literal sense of illegitimate children or those raised in misguidance, "children" also esoterically refers to the "progeny" of our actions, thoughts, and teachings. If one's deeds lean towards evil, or if one spreads misleading knowledge or guides others away from the true path, then Satan becomes a "partner" in these "offspring." This also represents individuals or disciples (spiritual "children") who find themselves "born" into spiritual ignorance or are led astray by focusing solely on the Zahir (literal interpretation) without grasping the Batin (inner meaning) as taught by the Imam. Furthermore, it signifies dedicating one's lineage or efforts to worldly goals instead of spiritual pursuits.

 

 "And promise them.' But Satan does not promise them except delusion ( غرور )" :

 This highlights the misleading character of satanic temptations. The so-called "promises" usually focus on worldly power, transient pleasures, and false securities, along with the allure of instant gratification. Ultimately, however, they can result in spiritual ruin and disillusionment. What constitutes promises? They are guarantees, offering a sense of security. One example of a satanic promise is the claim of worldly success; assurances that disobedience to Allah is the appropriate choice in a given situation. 

 The esoteric path invites us to pierce through these illusions and prioritize discovering eternal truths and embracing spiritual realities.

 

 So Allah (Imam Hunaid) laid out Iblis's plan as a warning for Adam.

 The Creation of Eve (Hawwa - حَوَّاءَ )

 Consider the concept of لا الہ الا اللہ, which juxtaposes negation with affirmation, the superior alongside the inferior, and male with female. The narration describes how Allah caused Adam to fall asleep, took one of his ribs, and created   حواء (Eve) from it. Humans possess 12 ribs on each side: the 12 on the right symbolize scholars endowed with subtle, lofty, and esoteric wisdom, while those on the left represent individuals who hold dense, physical, and argumentative knowledge. These ribs symbolize the support of the rib cage for the torso, just as these 24 نقباء (chiefs) support the foundation of religion. 

 

 Imam Hunaid (AS) chose one member of the twelve learned scholars to accompany Adam. This individual is known as حواء , a name derived from the root word حیی , which signifies life-giving or a source of life. Why? This is the Asaas's role in clarifying interpretations of the apparent Shariah established by the Natiq. Originally, Haris was meant to be the Asas of Adam. But since he refused Adam's leadership and was expelled from the presence of the Imam, he was replaced by another person who took the role of Hawwa.

 Imam Hunaid instructed Adam to educate and spiritually support Eve ( تائید ), thereby making him his companion and Hujjah (Proof). Imam Hunaid directed Adam to nurture, teach, and guide this individual, naming him as his wife and companion. The Arabic term زوج  translates to "partner." Haww wasn't actually a woman. He was a man from among the 12 حدود  of Adam who was made subordinate to Adam; this is why he is referred to as his wife in the Quran.

 Testing Adam & Eve

 The Quran states:

 

 The Cow  [2:35] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏وَقُلْنَا يَٓـَٔادَمُ ٱسْكُنْ أَنتَ وَزَوْجُكَ ٱلْجَنَّةَ وَكُلَا مِنْهَا رَغَدًا حَيْثُ شِئْتُمَا وَلَا تَقْرَبَا هَذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةَ فَتَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلظَّلِمِينَ ‎ ۳۵ 

 And We said: "O Adam! Dwell you and your wife in the Paradise and eat both of you freely with pleasure and delight of things therein as wherever you will, but come not near this tree or you both will be of the Zalimun  ."

 So Imam Hunaid (AS) tells Adam and Eve to enjoy the pleasures of paradise but then warns them not to approach a certain tree or they would become among the unjust. 

 

 Ta-Ha  [20:117] 

 [ سورۃ ۲۰] طہ 

 ‏فَقُلْنَا يَٓـَٔادَمُ إِنَّ هَذَا عَدُوٌّۭ لَّكَ وَلِزَوْجِكَ فَلَا يُخْرِجَنَّكُمَا مِنَ ٱلْجَنَّةِ فَتَشْقَىٰٓ ‎ ۱۱۷ 

 Then We said: "O Adam! Verily, this is an enemy to you and to your wife. So let him not get you both out of Paradise, so that you be distressed in misery.

 He also reminds them that Iblis is their main enemy, and they should be cautious of his provocations and tricks, as he aims to have them expelled from paradise. Imam Hunaid encourages both Adam and Eve to relish everything paradise has in store for them. In this context, paradise symbolizes the spiritual realm of knowledge and support ( تائید ) connected to the messengers and leaders. This represents the esteemed garment of paradise. What does a garment signify? It reflects an individual’s certain position or stature. Adam and Eve were blessed with this honor of divine support ( تائید ) from Imam Hunaid (AS), enabling them to grasp everything easily and respond to questions in a way that satisfied everyone. The trees of paradise were libraries filled with spiritual books, which Imam Hunaid made available to them. Adam and Hawwa began to experience this heavenly bliss of spiritual knowledge. The more they learned, the more the worldly pleasures became meaningless to them.

 Maulana Ali (AS) told Kumayl ibn Ziyad that knowledge surpasses wealth; wealth diminishes when spent, but knowledge expands when shared. Wealth hoarders die, but scholars live on through their impact.

 Forbidden Tree

 But then there were also very clearly warned:

 وَلَا تَقْرَبَا هَذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةَ فَتَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلظَّلِمِينَ 

 Avoid this one tree, or you risk becoming unjust. What do the garden and the forbidden tree symbolize? The garden represents Imam Hunaid's vast library, rich with various spiritual wisdom (or fruits). At the same time, the forbidden tree signifies a specific shelf containing books about the gnosis and knowledge of  قائم القیامۃ (The Resurrector) and his lofty ranks, anticipated to appear after this 7,000-year period. The profound insights in these texts were so advanced that physical obligations could not conceal them. In fact, this knowledge would void physical duties. 

 Everything Adam learned was related to inner understanding that could manifest in tangible, observable actions. Imam Hunaid forbade it not because the knowledge was harmful but because Adam and Hawwa were not ready for it, and the time for such revelations had not yet come, as the book contained information related to Kashf rather than Sitr. It's like trying to jump to the final chapter of a book without first reading the earlier ones. Imam Hunaid cautioned them: فَتَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلظَّلِمِينَ - you will be among the unjust. What is ظلم ? Injustice is putting things in the wrong place. 

 

 The Heights  [7:20-21] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏فَوَسْوَسَ لَهُمَا ٱلشَّيْطَنُ لِيُبْدِىَ لَهُمَا مَا وُۥرِىَ عَنْهُمَا مِن سَوْءَتِهِمَا وَقَالَ مَا نَهَىٰكُمَا رَبُّكُمَا عَنْ هَذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةِ إِلَّآ أَن تَكُونَا مَلَكَيْنِ أَوْ تَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلْخَلِدِينَ ‎ ۲۰ ‏وَقَاسَمَهُمَآ إِنِّى لَكُمَا لَمِنَ ٱلنَّصِحِينَ ‎ ۲۱ 

 ( 20 ) Then Satan tempted them in order to expose what was hidden of their nakedness. He said, “Your Lord has forbidden this tree to you only to prevent you from becoming angels or immortals.” ( 21 ) And he swore to them, “I am truly your sincere advisor.” 

 Iblis recognized this reality. He knew what was in those books. He crafted a misleading narrative about the boundaries and limits of what was allowed. He suggested that Imam Hunaid forbade their reading of those books because acquiring such knowledge would eliminate the physical obligations that demand effort and struggle forever. Consequently, they and their children would no longer have to labor in the physical realm. Essentially, they would transform into eternal beings, no longer dependent on the material world of actions. He said you both will always be able to reside in the presence of the Imam and bask in his spiritual knowledge as opposed to going to the islands ( جزائر ) and striving to spread the word of God. 

 

 The Heights  [7:22] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏فَدَلَّىٰهُمَا بِغُرُورٍۢ ۚ فَلَمَّا ذَاقَا ٱلشَّجَرَةَ بَدَتْ لَهُمَا سَوْءَتُهُمَا وَطَفِقَا يَخْصِفَانِ عَلَيْهِمَا مِن وَرَقِ ٱلْجَنَّةِ ۖ وَنَادَىٰهُمَا رَبُّهُمَآ أَلَمْ أَنْهَكُمَا عَن تِلْكُمَا ٱلشَّجَرَةِ وَأَقُل لَّكُمَآ إِنَّ ٱلشَّيْطَنَ لَكُمَا عَدُوٌّۭ مُّبِينٌۭ ‎ ۲۲ 

 So he brought about their fall through deception. And when they tasted of the tree, their nakedness was exposed to them, prompting them to cover themselves with leaves from Paradise. Then their Lord called out to them, “Did I not forbid you from that tree and ?did I not? tell you that Satan is your sworn enemy?”

 Loss Of Garments

 This is how both were misled. Adam transgressed the limits of the Speaker ( ناطق ) and stepped into the realm of Resurrection ( قائم ), which Imam Hunaid prohibited. They reached for a knowledge that was way beyond their station. This is the tasting from the forbidden tree. The word used here is  فَلَمَّا ذَاقَا when they "tasted," not when they ate. This is because they only read a small portion of the book, but that was enough to cause the damage. Imam Hunaid's divine support was severed from them the moment they did that. 

 This symbolizes the loss of their garments, revealing their private parts. Our genitals represent vulnerability, weakness, and shame, which are hidden by our clothing. Similarly, Adam and Eve (and the children of Adam) had spiritual shortcomings in their knowledge that were hidden by the constant light of divine support ( تائید ) symbolized by the garments of heaven. Since divine support does not align with sin, their disobedience severed the divine connection with Imam Hunaid, and the weaknesses in their knowledge (limitations of their physical or lower nature) were exposed. It's like when you do something wrong and get that inner feeling of knowing you've messed up, which is the "shame."  

 First, engaging with the forbidden text left them deeply confused and disoriented. Second, without the Imam's divine support, they lost their ability to comprehend and teach. Now, they can read but cannot grasp the meaning. They face questions they can no longer answer. 

 يَخْصِفَانِ عَلَيْهِمَا مِن وَرَقِ ٱلْجَنَّةِ 

 So they tried to cover up their private parts with leaves from other trees. 

 They began to cling to the remembrance of the light of wisdom to conceal their weaknesses.  This is quite a sad image! When they felt lost and exposed, they tried to grab onto any bits of wisdom or good memories of guidance they still had. Imagine you're in a dark room, and the light goes out. You'd immediately try to find a candle or a flashlight – any small light source you can remember having. These "leaves of wisdom" are like those small lights. They were trying to use these memories of wisdom to "conceal with it the shame of what had become apparent from them." In essence, they lost their honorable garments, and their vulnerability (i.e., private parts) was exposed to the world.

 

 The Heights  [7:23] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَا رَبَّنَا ظَلَمْنَآ أَنفُسَنَا وَإِن لَّمْ تَغْفِرْ لَنَا وَتَرْحَمْنَا لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ ٱلْخَسِرِينَ ‎ ۲۳ 

 They said: "Our Lord! We have wronged ourselves. If You forgive us not, and bestow not upon us Your Mercy, we shall certainly be of the losers."

 They said we had wronged ourselves by taking on a burden that was too great for our souls, and if we don't forgive ourselves and ask for mercy—meaning re-establish the divine connection—we will be among the losers.

 

 The Cow  [2:37] 

 [ سورۃ ۲] البقرۃ 

 ‏فَتَلَقَّىٰٓ آدَمُ مِن رَّبِّهِۦ كَلِمَتٍۢ فَتَابَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ هُوَ ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ ‎ ۳۷ 

 Then Adam received from his Lord Words. And his Lord pardoned him. ٰIndeed, He is the One Who forgives, the Most Merciful.

 He approached the Imam with a heart full of repentance, invoking the higher names he had learned from that book. He said, "O Imam, I reach out to you in the name of Maulana Ali and Qaim (AS). Forgive us through their exalted names." Imam Hunaid acknowledged their repentance, for He is truly forgiving and exceedingly merciful. This established the understanding that all Adam's children would err. However, those who persist in their sins without returning to Allah and who do not seek forgiveness through His chosen servants will belong to Iblis and his followers.

 

 The Heights  [7:24-25] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏قَالَ ٱهْبِطُوا۟ بَعْضُكُمْ لِبَعْضٍ عَدُوٌّۭ ۖ وَلَكُمْ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مُسْتَقَرٌّۭ وَمَتَعٌ إِلَىٰ حِينٍۢ ‎ ۲۴ ‏قَالَ فِيهَا تَحْيَوْنَ وَفِيهَا تَمُوتُونَ وَمِنْهَا تُخْرَجُونَ ‎ ۲۵ 

 ( 24 ) [ Allah ] said, "Descend, being to one another enemies. And for you on the earth is a place of settlement and enjoyment for a time." ( 25 ) He said: "Therein you shall live, and therein you shall die, and from it you shall be brought out." 

 Fall of Adam

 Imam Hunaid accepted Adam's repentance but didn't keep him in that state of direct, unveiled divine connection ( تائیدات ). He commanded them to descend. This descent wasn't a demotion from his rank as Natiq/Khalifah but a move from a state of unveiled interpretation to one where divine support ( مَادَّة ) comes veiled, descending through higher spiritual ranks ( حدود علویۃ ) to the lower ranks ( حدود سفلی ) who guide humanity. 

 This veiled transmission is illustrated by a hadith where Prophet Muhammad (PBUH) describes receiving revelation through a chain: Pen -> Tablet -> Israfil -> Mika'il -> Jibril -> Prophet. This shows knowledge descending through levels. Five higher spiritual ranks transmit down to five lower earthly ranks:

 

 Higher Ranks قلم لوح اسرافیل میکائیل جبرئیل Lower Ranks داعی حجۃ امام اساس ناطق 

 Allah still provided guidance: 

 

 The Heights  [7:26] 

 [ سورۃ ۷] الاعراف 

 ‏يَبَنِىٓ آدَمَ قَدْ أَنزَلْنَا عَلَيْكُمْ لِبَاسًۭا يُوَرِى سَوْءَتِكُمْ وَرِيشًۭا ۖ وَلِبَاسُ ٱلتَّقْوَىٰ ذَلِكَ خَيْرٌۭ ۚ ذَلِكَ مِنْ آيَتِ ٱللَّهِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَذَّكَّرُونَ ‎ ۲۶ 

 O Children of Adam! We have bestowed raiment upon you to cover yourselves and as an adornment, and the raiment of righteousness, that is better. Such are among the Ayat  of Allah, that they may remember.

 O Children of Adam, we have granted you this "divine support" through the spiritual hierarchy to fortify your soul and adorn it for the afterlife. The entire narrative of Adam and Eve—creation, gender roles, obedience versus disobedience, repentance versus persistence, and the choice between Allah's followers or Iblis's followers—resonates through Adam's descendants. Those who align themselves with Iblis spiritually detach from Adam's genuine lineage. The seven gates of Hell are tied to the rejection of the seven great Messengers/Speakers and their evident teachings. Maulana Ali (AS) is referred to as the قَيِّمُ ٱلنَّارِ "overseer of the Fire," indicating his role in distinguishing between those destined for it and those who are saved.

 

 

 Adam's Descent and the Hierarchy 

 

 Continuing Struggle: Iblis remains the enemy. Those who follow Allah's guides (Natiq, Samit, Imam, Hujjah) are in Adam's party (Allah's party). Those who oppose them are in Iblis's party. Forgetting Allah (ذِكْرَ ٱللَّهِ - Dhikr Allah) here means being turned away from His guides, who are His living remembrance ( dhikr ).

 Pattern Repeats: The whole story of Adam and Eve – creation, male/female, obedience/disobedience, repentance/persistence, joining Allah's party or Iblis's party – repeats throughout their descendants. Those who ally with Iblis spiritually leave Adam's true lineage. Q|5:51 Q|11:46 (Regarding Noah's son) The seven gates of Hell are linked to rejecting the seven great Messengers/Speakers and their laws (apparent teachings). Maulana Ali (AS) is mentioned as the "overseer of the Fire," meaning he distinguishes between those destined for it and those saved.

 Succession After Adam 

 Adam was supported by Allah's testament/guidance (ٱلْوَصِيَّةِ - Al-Wasiyyah) and established as the Imam on Earth.

 He appointed his son Abel as his successor ( Wasi ).

 His other son, Cain, became jealous and killed Abel, persisting in sin like Iblis.

 Allah gifted Adam another son, Seth (شِيث - Shith), meaning "Gift of Allah" (هِبَةَ ٱللَّهِ - Hibatullah). Adam secretly passed the knowledge and succession to Seth, telling him to conceal it ( taqiyyah - تَقِيَّةً) from Cain. This started the tradition of protecting the inner truths.

 After Adam passed, Seth became the Imam. The chain continued through قائم after قائم (Qaim after Qaim) and Imam after Imam.

 Idris (AS) is mentioned as being from Adam's era ( dawr - دَوْر).

 This cycle ( dawr ) of Adam ended when Noah (AS) was sent as a new Speaker (Natiq) with a new law ( shari'ah - شَرِيعَة). Q|5:48

 Each new Prophet first upholds the previous law before bringing the new one, based on the covenant Allah took from them. Q|33:7

 Final Advice 

 Finally, I understand the advice given to believers (like me):

 Understand: Grasp the inner interpretation ( ta’wil ) of the Quran. Know that this deep knowledge is with Allah and the "Firm in Knowledge" (ٱلرَّاسِخُونَ فِى ٱلْعِلْمِ - Ar-Rasikhuna fil-‘Ilm), who are His Awliya (friends/guides/Imams).

 Be Grateful: Appreciate the blessing of having these guides, whose guardianship ( wilayah - وَلَايَة) makes us part of Allah's party and saves us from Iblis's party.

 Act: Fulfill Allah's commands outwardly ( zahir ) and inwardly ( batin ) with sincerity.

 Repent: If you sin, repent sincerely to Allah. Seek the Imam of your time ( Wali Zaman ) so he can seek forgiveness for you, and seek forgiveness yourself. This way, you rejoin the party of Adam and Allah.

 Avoid Despair: Don't be like Iblis who persisted in sin and despaired. Don't lose hope in Allah's mercy. Q|12:87 Q|15:56

 Prayers: The explanation concludes with prayers for success in pleasing Allah, protection from sin, openness to obeying the Awliya , and blessings on Prophet Muhammad (PBUH), his family, and the pure Imams.

 Explanation of Additional Terms 

 Based on the context of this text, which seems rooted in Ismaili thought, here's my understanding of the terms you asked about:

 Periods (أَدْوَار - Adwar): These are major cycles of religious history. Each period is usually initiated by a Speaker (Natiq) like Adam, Noah, Abraham, Moses, Jesus, and Muhammad (PBUH), who brings a new divine law (Shari'ah). Each Natiq is followed by a Foundation/Silent One (Asas/Samit), like Seth or Maulana Ali (AS), and a line of Imams who guard and interpret the law's outer (zahir) and inner (batin) meanings. The "دور (dawr) of Adam" is mentioned as ending when Noah began his mission.

 Kashf (كَشْف - Unveiling): This means unveiling, discovery, or spiritual insight. It refers to the direct understanding or experience of hidden, inner truths (Batin). The state before Adam and Eve's transgression, or the future state during the time of the Qa'im, seems closer to a state of Kashf where the inner reality is more apparent.

 Sitr (سِتْر - Concealment): This is the opposite of Kashf. It means veiling or concealing, particularly the inner truths (Batin). During most historical periods (Adwar), the inner meaning is deliberately concealed (Mastur - مَسْتُور) and protected by the Imam and his Hujjah (the Samit). Only initiates or those capable receive this knowledge gradually. Seth was commanded to conceal (كِتْمَان - Kitman) the knowledge. Taqiyyah (prudent concealment) is related to this.

 Imam Hunaid: This specific name, "Imam Hunaid," does not appear in the provided text. The concept of the Imam is discussed extensively as the rightful leader and guide after a Prophet or the previous Imam, holding both apparent (Zahir) and inner (Batin) authority and knowledge, tracing the lineage back to Adam. Perhaps "Hunaid" refers to a specific figure in a particular historical context or tradition not covered here, or it could be a misunderstanding or variation of another name (like Junayd, a famous Sufi, though this text's context is Ismaili). Within this explanation, the focus is on the major figures like Adam, the Nutuqa', and the general concept of the Imamate.
